*Hao Guo,Subin Huang,Junjie Chen,Zhifa Geng,Sanmin Liu,Chao Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种多模态讽刺检测框架，通过动态门控跨模态融合与讽刺感知对比正则化（SaCR）来应对实例相关的模态贡献和具有误导性的语义一致性问题，在两个基准数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态讽刺检测方法依赖固定的融合策略，将讽刺视为通用的跨模态不匹配，无法捕捉实例特定的模态交互和细微的讽刺线索；同时，表面语义一致性可能掩盖潜在的矛盾意图，导致检测困难。

Method: 提出新颖的MSD框架，包含：(1) 双向门控交互模块，执行跨模态特征过滤并在实例级别自适应校准文本与视觉贡献；(2) 动态融合门控，平衡模态重要性以生成鲁棒的多模态表示；(3) 讽刺感知对比正则化（SaCR），作为标签感知的对比正则化目标，对非讽刺样本鼓励语义一致性同时抑制讽刺样本的误导性一致性；(4) 端到端的多目标学习策略，联合优化多模态分类和辅助单模态监督。

Result: 在MMSD和MMSD2.0两个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，所提出的方法在性能上持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过实例级别的自适应模态调权与讽刺感知对比正则化的结合，能更有效地建模讽刺语义，提升多模态讽刺检测的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multimodal sarcasm detection aims to identify sarcastic intent from multimodal content, where inconsistencies between literal meaning and contextual cues often signal irony. This task has attracted increasing research attention. However, accurate detection remains challenging due to instance-dependent modality contributions and misleading semantic consistency, where surface-level alignment masks underlying contradictory intent. Existing methods often rely on fixed fusion strategies and treat sarcasm as generic cross-modal mismatch, limiting their ability to capture subtle sarcasm cues and instance-specific modality interactions. To address these challenges, we propose a novel MSD framework that integrates Dynamic Gated Cross-Modal Fusion with Sarcastic-aware Contrastive Regularization (SaCR). Specifically, a bidirectional gated interaction module performs cross-modal feature filtering and adaptively calibrates textual and visual contributions at the instance level. A dynamic fusion gate further balances modality importance to generate more robust multimodal representations. Furthermore, SaCR is introduced as a label-aware contrastive regularization objective that encourages semantic consistency for non-sarcastic samples while suppressing misleading consistency in sarcastic cases. The proposed framework is trained end-to-end with a multi-objective learning strategy that jointly optimizes multimodal classification and auxiliary unimodal supervision. Extensive experiments on MMSD and MMSD2.0 demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms strong baselines.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了1C:Enterprise生态系统的自然语言代码检索完整方案，包括包含3,413个真实查询-代码对的开源基准、可复现的评估框架，以及基于784,057个合成三元组微调的专业bi-encoder模型。该模型在平衡宏nDCG@10上达到0.5992，并通过Matryoshka表示学习在256维截断时保留99.9%的检索质量。

Abstract: Natural language code retrieval is a rapidly evolving task in computer science. However, the 1C:Enterprise ecosystem combines Russian syntax with highly domain-specific terminology, for which open datasets and specialized models have been virtually non-existent. We present a comprehensive pipeline for 1C code retrieval: an open benchmark of 3,413 real-world, PII-scrubbed query-code pairs, a reproducible evaluation harness, and a specialized bi-encoder. To overcome scarce labeled data, we fine-tune on 784,057 synthetic triplets generated by google/gemma-4-26B-A4B-it from public code repositories, using Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) and a privacy-aware tokenizer. Because the benchmark subsets differ in size, we report balanced-subset macro, query-weighted micro, and forum-only results. Our model reaches 0.5992 balanced macro nDCG@10, 0.5044 micro, and 0.4617 on forum, versus 0.4932 macro for the baseline architecture and 0.5404 for google/embeddinggemma-300m. Removing every benchmark example flagged by the conservative exact/13-gram overlap audit leaves 0.6011 balanced macro (0.5010 micro), indicating that detected train-benchmark overlap does not explain the headline result. MRL truncation to 256 dimensions preserves 99.9% of retrieval quality while reducing dense-index storage and exact similarity arithmetic by a factor of three.

</details>


### [38] [Robust Incomplete Multimodal Sentiment Analysis via Iterative Proxy Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19971)
*Zhifa Geng,Subin Huang,Hao Guo,Junjie Chen,Sanmin Liu,Chao Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种迭代代理校正框架（IPCF）用于鲁棒的不完整多模态情感分析，通过从非语言模态构造语言代理，并在多模态上下文中通过门控残差校正逐步细化代理，再根据估计的语言可靠性分数自适应融合，从而解决了传统代理方法一次性构造后过早注入导致误差传播的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景中多模态输入常不完整或受损，削弱跨模态互补性并引入误导信息。现有基于代理的方法依赖一次性代理构造来补偿劣化的语言信息，但生成的代理粗糙且不可靠，过早注入多模态推理会传播初始误差并损害情感预测。

Method: 1) 从非语言模态构造语言导向代理；2) 通过门控残差校正机制在多模态上下文中迭代细化代理；3) 基于估计的语言可靠性分数将校正后的代理与观测语言表征自适应融合；4) 引入分阶段潜在校正目标，利用完整语言表征作为训练时语义锚点以稳定代理细化轨迹。

Result: 在MOSI、MOSEI和SIMS数据集上，针对多种缺失模态设置的大量实验表明，所提框架一致优于竞争基线，在不完整输入下实现鲁棒的情感预测。

Conclusion: 该迭代代理校正框架有效解决了传统代理方法初始误差传播问题，通过渐进式细化和可靠性感知的融合策略，显著提升了不完整多模态情感分析的鲁棒性，在多个基准数据集上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis aims to infer affective states by integrating language, visual, and acoustic cues. However, real-world multimodal inputs are often incomplete or corrupted, which can weaken cross-modal complementarity and introduce misleading information into downstream fusion. Existing proxy-based methods for incomplete MSA commonly rely on one-shot proxy construction to compensate for degraded language information, but the generated proxy may be coarse or unreliable at initialization. Prematurely injecting such a proxy into multimodal reasoning can propagate initial errors and compromise sentiment prediction. To address this limitation, we propose an iterative proxy correction framework for robust incomplete MSA. Our method constructs a language-oriented proxy from non-language modalities and progressively refines it under multimodal context through gated residual correction. The corrected proxy is then adaptively fused with the observed language representation according to an estimated language reliability score, allowing the model to balance proxy-based compensation and trustworthy linguistic evidence. In addition, we introduce a stage-wise latent correction objective that uses the complete language representation as a training-time semantic anchor to stabilize the proxy refinement trajectory. Extensive experiments on MOSI, MOSEI, and SIMS under diverse missing-modality settings demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms competitive baselines and achieves robust sentiment prediction under incomplete inputs.

</details>

*Yingjian Chen,Fan Gao,Sherry T. Tong,Haoyu Zhang,Aosong Feng,Kevin W. Jin,Xing Wu,Jinghui Lu,Abdul Samad,Akbar Faruqi,Cesar Caraballo,Cibele Brandão,Dhruva,Gupta,Eunji Jeon,Gabriel Madera-Santiago,Geon Lee,Hugo Toshio Itikawa,Insook Cho,Isabelli Martins,Isarar Siddique,Israr Ahmed,Jihyo Kwak,Kanyakorn Veerakanjana,Luis Guilherme Cardoso,Minjin Kim,Piyalitt Ittichaiwong,Renee Dua,Santiago Gudiño-Rosales,Xiujie Chen,Zeo Lapalus,Zixin Xu,Michihiro Yasunaga,Rex Ying,Heuiseok Lim,Jaewoo Kang,Chanjun Park,Hang Jiang,Ethan Goh,Hyunjae Kim,Edison Marrese-Taylor,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Qingyu Chen,Irene Li*

TL;DR: HealMed是一个由23位医学专家历时两年构建的多语言医学评测基准，包含9种语言各1000个样本，覆盖MCQA、NLI和开放式问答三种任务格式。研究发现：低资源语言上模型性能下降最明显；最强的专有模型跨语言稳定性最好，而许多开源和医学专用模型表现出更大且不一致的差距；医学专业化本身并不能保证多语言鲁棒性；专家修订翻译会显著影响评测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学领域的大语言模型评测大多集中于英语，缺乏经过专家审核的多语言基准来可靠评估模型在不同语言下的医学能力，尤其是低资源语言场景下的表现。

Method: 构建包含9,000个样本（9种语言×1,000个）的多语言医学评测基准HealMed，数据来源涵盖9个数据集、3种任务格式（MCQA、NLI、开放式问答）。由9个国家和地区23名医学专家历时两年开发，每份翻译由两位精通英语和目标语言的专家评估修订。

Result: 模型在低资源语言上性能下降最大，但下降幅度因语言和模型而异。最强的专有模型跨语言最稳定，而许多开源和医学专用模型存在更大且不一致的差距。医学专用模型并不比通用模型更具多语言鲁棒性。专家修订翻译既可能提升也可能降低模型性能，说明翻译质量对跨语言评测结果有实质性影响。

Conclusion: HealMed揭示了多语言医学评测中语言资源与模型稳定性之间的关键关系，强调翻译质量评估是跨语言基准构建中不可忽视的环节，医学专用化并不等同于多语言能力的增强。

Abstract: We present HealMed, an expert-reviewed benchmark for multilingual evaluation of large language models in medicine. HealMed contains 1,000 examples in each of nine languages, drawn from nine datasets and covering three task formats: MCQA, NLI and open-ended QA. The benchmark was developed over two years by 23 physicians and medical experts based across nine countries and regions. Each translation was evaluated and revised by two experts fluent in English and the corresponding target language. On HealMed, performance declined most in low-resource languages, although the size of the gap varied markedly across languages and models. The strongest proprietary models were the most stable across languages, whereas many open-source and medically specialized models showed larger and less consistent gaps. Medical specialization alone did not ensure multilingual robustness. Furthermore, expert revision could either raise or lower measured performance, indicating that translation quality materially affects cross-language evaluation results.

</details>


### [40] [Auditing Cross-Lingual Fairness in Language Model Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20047)
*Alexander Nemecek,Osama Zafar,Debargha Ganguly,Vikash Singh,Vipin Chaudhary,Erman Ayday*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于大型语言模型水印技术的多语言评估框架，包含四个组件：经验校准的检测阈值、与阈值无关的伴随测量、三种不重叠的质量测量范式，以及基于广义熵的跨语言差异分解。在六个水印方案、三个生成器、十一种语言和两种微调模式下验证，发现跨语言公平性差距主要源于语言类型学结构属性而非特定语言的特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型水印方案几乎仅在英文文本上评估，使用单一的检测阈值和狭窄的质量度量。多语言部署会暴露这些在英文上无碍但在跨语言场景下决定结论的评估设计选择，因此需要一套系统的多语言评估框架。

Method: 提出四组件评估框架：(1) 按部署情境经验校准的检测阈值；(2) 与阈值无关的伴随测量以区分校准失败与检测失败；(3) 三种互补的质量测量范式（分布级、配对语义级、参考困惑度）；(4) 基于广义熵分解的跨语言差异分析，按类型学语系划分。在六个水印方案、三个开源生成器、十一语言（四脚本、八类型学语系）及基础/指令微调两种模式下进行系统实验。

Result: 该框架揭示了单语言单范式评估无法暴露的失败模式。检测和质量两方面的观察差异主要落在类型学语系分区之间，表明水印的跨语言公平性差距是语言结构属性所决定的，而非特定语言的偶发现象。

Conclusion: 多语言水印评估需要系统化的框架设计，跨语言公平性差距是语言类型学结构属性的系统性结果，单语言单范式的评估不足以支持多语言部署决策。

Abstract: Watermarking schemes for large language model output are evaluated almost exclusively on English text using each scheme's detection threshold and a narrow set of quality measurements. Multilingual deployment exposes evaluation-design choices that are inconsequential on English but determine conclusions cross-lingually. We propose an evaluation framework with four components: detection thresholds calibrated empirically per deployment context, a threshold-independent companion measurement that distinguishes calibration failures from detection failures, three disjoint quality measurement paradigms (distributional, paired-semantic, and reference-perplexity), and a generalized-entropy decomposition of cross-language disparity over a typological family partition. Applied to six watermarking schemes, three open-weight generators, eleven languages spanning four scripts and eight typological families, and both base and instruction-tuned regimes, the framework reveals failure modes that single-language single-paradigm evaluation cannot surface. Across detection and quality, observed disparity is predominantly between-family on the typological partition, indicating that cross-lingual fairness gaps in watermarking are structural to language properties rather than idiosyncratic to particular languages.

</details>


### [41] [SABET-QA: Temporal Knowledge Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20083)
*Brahim Touayouch,Mirette Moawad,Dmitry Akulov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SABET-QA框架，通过迭代多跳推理、双向实体-时间评分和槽位感知上下文模块，解决时间知识图谱问答中嵌入方法在复杂多步查询上推理不足的问题，在多个基准数据集上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于嵌入的时间知识图谱问答方法采用单通道推理流水线，难以处理多步查询；时间敏感事实的推理需要多跳迭代的机制来逐步细化推理状态。

Method: SABET-QA包含三个核心组件：(1) 双向实体-时间评分机制，用于多跳迭代中评估实体与时间的关系；(2) 槽位感知上下文化模块，对齐问题语义与时间KG嵌入；(3) 可微工作记忆，支持逐步假设精炼；辅以时间边界作为粗粒度监督信号。

Result: 在CronQuestions、Complex-CronQuestions、MultiTQ和TimeQuestions四个基准数据集上均优于强基线，尤其在复杂多步时间查询上提升显著。

Conclusion: 迭代多跳推理与双向时间感知评分相结合的方法能有效克服单通道推理的局限，显著提升时间知识图谱问答在复杂多步查询上的性能。

Abstract: Question Answering over Temporal Knowledge Graphs (TKGQA) requires reasoning over time-sensitive facts, yet existing embedding-based methods struggle with multi-step queries due to single-pass reasoning pipelines. We propose SABET-QA, a framework that iteratively refines reasoning states across multiple hops via a bidirectional entity-temporal scoring mechanism and a slot-aware contextualization module that aligns question semantics with temporal KG embeddings. A differentiable working memory enables progressive hypothesis refinement, while auxiliary temporal boundaries serve as coarse supervision when available. Experiments on CronQuestions, Complex-CronQuestions, MultiTQ, and TimeQuestions demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines, particularly on complex multi-step temporal queries.

</details>


### [42] [OenoBench: A Wine-Domain Benchmark for Knowledge-Grounded Evaluation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20106)
*Nikita Khudov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了OenoBench，一个包含3,266道葡萄酒领域多选题的知识基准，涵盖六大支柱和四个难度等级，采用LLM驱动的构建管线（LLM仅负责格式化与审计，绝不作为事实来源），对16个前沿模型配置进行评估，发现准确率范围53%-84%，揭示了推理模式增益不均、模型自我偏好、参数化回忆天花板等关键现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 葡萄酒领域缺乏系统化、事实可溯源的领域知识基准。现有基准常依赖LLM直接生成内容，导致事实来源不可靠；同时需要评估前沿模型在专业领域知识上的真实表现，特别是推理模式、模型偏好和开放权重模型的性价比。

Method: 从38,104条原子事实出发（来自35个经来源验证的爬虫，覆盖政府注册机构、同行评审期刊和Wikipedia/Wikidata），构建3,266道多选题。核心方法论贡献是LLM驱动的管线：LLM仅负责重述已验证事实和审计结果，但绝不作为事实来源；每道题由五种生成策略之一生成（覆盖五个生成器族），经九代理审计并通过与人工标注（Cohen's κ）校准。评估了16个前沿模型配置，涵盖推理模式、自我偏好、开放权重模型等多个维度。

Result: (i) 总体准确率范围53%-84%，o3以83.6%领先；(ii) 推理模式增益集中在DeepSeek R1（+6.8pp），在Claude Opus和Gemini Pro上无增益；(iii) Anthropic对其自生成问题有+9pp自我偏好，Google有-8pp反向偏好；(iv) 开放权重前沿模型与专有推理模型共享成本-准确率帕累托前沿；(v) 所有配置在封闭书可解题目上增益约33pp，揭示了仅上下文切片可规避的参数化回忆天花板。

Conclusion: OenoBench提供了一个严格的事实溯源导向的领域知识基准构建范式，证明LLM可以安全地参与基准构建而非仅作为数据来源。评估结果揭示了前沿模型的推理模式效果不均、自我偏好偏差以及参数化知识回忆的上限，这些发现对基础模型的能力评估和部署决策具有重要参考价值。数据集、审计发现、评审应用和构建代码以CC-BY-SA-4.0协议对外发布。

Abstract: We introduce OenoBench, a wine-domain knowledge benchmark of 3,266 multiple-choice questions across six pillars (regions, grape varieties, viticulture, winemaking, producers, business) and four difficulty tiers. The corpus is built from 38,104 atomic, source-anchored facts extracted by 35 provenance-verified scrapers from government registries (INAO, TTB, OIV), peer-reviewed journals, and Wikipedia/Wikidata. Our methodological contribution is an LLM-driven pipeline in which language models reformat verified facts and audit the result, but never serve as the source of truth: every claim traces to a URL, every question is generated by one of five strategies across five generator families, and every question is scored by a nine-agent audit calibrated against a human gold sheet via Cohen's $κ$. Evaluating sixteen frontier configurations, we find: (i) overall accuracy spans 53%-84%, led by o3 at 83.6%; (ii) reasoning-mode lift concentrates in DeepSeek R1 (+6.8pp) and is absent in Claude Opus and Gemini Pro; (iii) Anthropic shows +9pp self preference on its own questions while Google shows -8pp inverse preference; (iv) frontier open-weight models share the cost-vs-accuracy Pareto frontier with proprietary reasoning models; and (v) every config gains around 33pp on closed-book solvable items, revealing a parametric-recall ceiling that only the contextual slice avoids. We release corpus, audit findings, human-review app, and construction code under CC-BY-SA-4.0.

</details>


### [43] [When Text and Numbers Disagree: Evidence Arbitration in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20116)
*Mattia Carletti,Edward Phillips,Fredrik K. Gustafsson,Patitapaban Palo,Lei Clifton,Danielle Belgrave,Xiao Gu,David A. Clifton*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过一个受控的合成基准研究了LLM在文本摘要、数值时间序列和外部工具输出等冲突证据之间进行仲裁的行为。研究发现模型在文本与数值之间存在系统性偏好，对时间近因性的遵循程度高于显式可靠性线索，且可能过度依赖外部预测，揭示了LLM在工具增强决策系统中依赖启发式仲裁策略的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用于需要综合处理多源异构证据（如文本摘要、数值观测、外部工具输出）的场景，而这些来源可能提供相互矛盾的证据。理解LLM如何在这些冲突来源之间进行仲裁，对于构建可靠的工具增强决策系统至关重要，但目前缺乏系统性的研究。

Method: 作者构建了一个受控的合成基准，其中潜在风险轨迹同时生成数值时间序列和自然语言摘要，并构造恰好一个证据来源与真实标签一致的冲突场景。该设计允许独立操控模态（文本vs数值）、时间近因性、来源可靠性和证据出处等因素，并基于此对多种开源指令微调模型进行系统评测。

Result: 研究发现：1）LLM在证据仲裁中表现出系统性而非随机的行为；2）模型在文本与数值之间存在明显偏好差异；3）模型对时间近因性的遵循比显式可靠性线索更一致；4）模型可能过度依赖外部预测，即使这些预测与直接上下文证据冲突。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在整合异构证据时往往依赖启发式仲裁策略而非深层推理，这构成了工具增强决策系统的一个关键失败模式，需要在未来加以改进。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in settings where textual summaries, numerical observations, and external tool outputs may provide conflicting evidence. We study how LLMs arbitrate between such sources when they support opposing decisions. To do so, we introduce a controlled synthetic benchmark in which latent risk trajectories generate both numerical time series and natural language summaries, allowing us to construct conflicts where exactly one evidence source is aligned with the ground-truth label. This design lets us independently manipulate modality, temporal recency, source reliability, and evidence provenance. Across open-weight instruction-tuned models, we find that arbitration behaviour is systematic rather than random: models exhibit distinct text-versus-number preferences, follow temporal recency more consistently than explicit reliability cues, and can over-rely on external forecasts even when they conflict with direct contextual evidence. These results suggest that current LLMs often rely on heuristic arbitration strategies when integrating heterogeneous evidence, highlighting a failure mode for tool-augmented decision systems.

</details>


### [44] [FormalTCS: Benchmarking End-to-End Frontier Formal Theoretical Computer Science Research of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20153)
*Dingzirui Wang,Xuanliang Zhang,Keyan Xu,Qingfu Zhu,Wanxiang Che*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了\ourbenchmark，一个由专家验证的基准测试，用于评估LLM在前沿、端到端的理论计算机科学（TCS）研究上的能力。基准包含175个来自2025-2026年STOC、FOCS、SODA和COLT论文的实例，评估显示现有LLM远未可靠地完成完整研究流程，其中自动形式化是最严重的瓶颈，且除形式化外，有限的研究品味（research taste）是自主TCS研究的另一大障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试与真实研究场景差距较大，缺乏对LLM在前沿、端到端TCS研究能力进行公平、专家级评估的工具。

Method: 构建包含175个实例的专家验证基准\ourbenchmark，实例来自2025-2026年STOC/FOCS/SODA/COLT论文，保留论文特定定义、假设和证明依赖，并使用专家验证的Lean形式化及证明。在此基础上开发一个自动化TCS研究框架，该框架可生成、形式化、过滤并证明新论断。

Result: 对领先LLM的评估显示，最优模型在将自然语言论断翻译为形式化定理陈述上仅得11.5分（对比在人类提供形式化陈述上证明的Pass@8=28.6），表明自动形式化是最尖锐的瓶颈。在自动化研究框架中，64个生成的论断最终仅有6个通过专家评估和证明验证。

Conclusion: 当前LLM无法可靠完成端到端TCS研究流程；除形式化能力外，有限的研究品味（即能否提出有价值的论断）是自主TCS研究的另一主要障碍。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown growing potential for automated theoretical computer science (TCS) research, yet existing benchmarks remain far from realistic research settings. We introduce \ourbenchmark, an expert-validated benchmark for evaluating LLMs on frontier, end-to-end TCS research. \ourbenchmark contains $175$ instances drawn from papers accepted to STOC, FOCS, SODA, and COLT in 2025-2026, preserving paper-specific definitions, assumptions, and proof dependencies, with expert-verified Lean formalizations and proofs. Evaluations of leading LLMs reveal that current models remain far from reliably completing the full research pipeline. In particular, autoformalization is the sharpest bottleneck: the best model achieves only $11.5$ on translating natural-language claims into formal theorem statements, compared with $28.6$ Pass@8 when proving human-provided formal statements. Building on \ourbenchmark, we further develop an automated TCS research framework that generates, formalizes, filters, and proves new claims. Of $64$ generated claims, only $6$ ultimately pass expert evaluation and proof verification, indicating that beyond formalization, limited research taste remains another major barrier to autonomous TCS research.

</details>


### [45] [Task-CoEvolve: Efficient Harness Optimization via Adaptive Validation Task Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20169)
*Atsuyuki Miyai,Kiyoharu Aizawa,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Task-CoEvolve方法，通过自适应验证任务选择来优化LLM agent的harness代码。该方法利用任务间候选harness的分歧度进行方差加权采样，聚焦于agent能力边界附近的任务，并校正采样偏差来估计全量集性能。在在线文本分类和Terminal-Bench 2.1上，将优化期间评估次数减少80%的同时匹配全量集搜索的最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的harness优化方法在每次迭代中都在完整的固定验证集上评估，即使某些任务随着harness演进变得不再具有区分度，仍会产生高昂的评估成本，严重限制了优化效率。

Method: Task-CoEvolve的核心包含两个关键机制：(1)基于候选harness在任务上的分歧度进行信息量判断，采用方差加权采样，根据历史结果将评估集中在agent能力边界附近的任务上，采样分布随harness演进而自适应调整；(2)利用采样概率对部分评估结果进行校正，从而估计全量集得分，确保不同迭代间评估结果可比。

Result: 在在线文本分类和Terminal-Bench 2.1两个基准上的实验表明，Task-CoEvolve一致优于固定子集基线，在将优化期间评估次数减少80%的情况下，匹配了全量集搜索的最终性能。

Conclusion: 通过将验证任务与harness协同演进，可以在不牺牲最终性能的前提下大幅降低harness优化的评估成本，为LLM agent的高效优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: We present a novel approach to efficient LLM agent harness optimization through adaptive validation task selection. Harness optimization iteratively rewrites the harness code based on validation performance, enabling substantial performance gains without updating the underlying model weights. Existing approaches, however, evaluate a fixed validation set in full at every iteration, incurring substantial evaluation costs even on tasks that become less discriminative as the harness evolves. We propose $\textbf{Task-CoEvolve}$, which co-evolves the validation tasks with the harness by addressing two challenges: selecting informative tasks and estimating full-set performance from partial evaluations. Task-CoEvolve builds on the observation that tasks on which candidate harnesses disagree are more informative for distinguishing among them than tasks that are consistently solved or failed. It uses variance-weighted sampling based on past outcomes to focus evaluation on tasks near the agent's capability frontier, with the sampling distribution adapting as the harness evolves. It then estimates full-set scores from the sampled tasks by accounting for their sampling probabilities, enabling consistent comparisons across iterations despite evaluating different subsets. Experiments on online text classification and Terminal-Bench 2.1 show that Task-CoEvolve consistently outperforms fixed-subset baselines and matches the final performance of full-set search while reducing the number of evaluations during optimization by 80%. Code will be released at https://github.com/Agent4Science-UTokyo/Task-CoEvolve.

</details>


### [46] [Inject, Align, Recover: Staged Post-Training for Retrieval-Free Document Knowledge Internalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20281)
*Qian Kou,Xiaofeng Shi,Xiaosong Qiu,Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出IAR（Inject, Align, and Recover）三阶段后训练框架，用于将固定文档语料库转化为可用的参数化知识，实现免检索的文档问答。不同于传统持续预训练，IAR将注入目标分解为续写、改写和指令条件重建，再通过答案-only监督对齐QA行为，最后与基础指令模型合并以恢复通用能力。在多个数据集和模型家族上，IAR在域内QA准确率上平均提升3.6个百分点，在通用性能上平均提升12.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在推理时若未检索源文档，往往难以回答关于有界文档集合的问题。现有方法（如传统持续预训练）未能有效将固定语料转化为参数化知识，导致零检索问答性能不佳。作者旨在解决文档知识内化问题，即在不大幅损失通用能力的前提下，将文档知识稳定地编码进模型参数中。

Method: 提出IAR三阶段后训练框架：(1) Inject阶段——不再使用传统持续预训练，而是将源文档转换为续写(continuation)、改写(rewrite)和指令条件重建(instruction-conditioned reconstruction)三种目标进行知识注入；(2) Align阶段——使用答案-only的QA监督信号适配注入后的模型，使其对齐问答行为；(3) Recover阶段——将领域适配后的模型与基础指令模型进行合并，恢复模型在通用任务上的能力。

Result: 在Common Corpus (CC)和CCI数据集上，跨Llama、Phi、Qwen、SmolLM四个模型家族验证：主对比中，IAR在8组数据集-模型设置中的7组上四项指标均优于Vanilla SFT，域内QA准确率平均提升3.6个百分点，IFEval、MMLU、MSBench上的平均通用性能提升12.1个百分点。扩展CC基线表明LoRA和FAPM可在个别通用指标上胜出，但在同时达到领先或接近领先的域内知识内化效果的方法中，IAR保持最强的通用性能profile之一。

Conclusion: IAR通过将知识注入、行为对齐和能力恢复三个阶段解耦，在文档知识内化任务上同时取得了优秀的域内QA性能和通用能力保持，验证了与传统持续预训练不同的分阶段策略在免检索文档问答中的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models often fail to answer questions about a bounded document collection when the source documents are not retrieved at inference time. We study this setting as document knowledge internalization: converting a fixed corpus into usable parametric knowledge for retrieval-free question answering. We propose IAR (Inject, Align, and Recover), a three-stage post-training framework that separates structured document knowledge injection, QA behavior alignment, and general ability recovery. Unlike conventional continued pretraining, Inject converts source documents into continuation, rewrite, and instruction-conditioned reconstruction objectives. Align then adapts the injected model with answer-only QA supervision, while Recover merges the domain-adapted model with the base instruction model to recover general capabilities. Across Common Corpus (CC) and CCI, and across Llama, Phi, Qwen, and SmolLM model families, IAR improves the domain-primary domain-general frontier for retrieval-free document internalization. In the main comparison, IAR improves over Vanilla SFT on all four reported metrics in 7 of 8 dataset-model settings, with average gains of 3.6 percentage points in domain QA accuracy and 12.1 percentage points in mean general performance across IFEval, MMLU, and MSBench. Extended CC baselines show that LoRA and FAPM can win individual general metrics, but among methods that also reach leading or near-leading domain internalization, IAR retains one of the strongest general profiles.

</details>


### [47] [Inducing Task Models from Computer-Use Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20319)
*Yucheng Jiang,Zora Zhiruo Wang,Ruishi Chen,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出任务模型归纳（TMI）方法，从自然主义的人机交互轨迹中自动发现潜在任务并归纳出结构化的任务模型（包括分层目标模型和程序控制流模型），在恢复交错任务和重建执行步骤方面显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然主义计算机使用轨迹中蕴含了如何完成日常工作的符号化、可审计、可复用的模型知识，对计算机使用代理进入真实工作场景至关重要。但现有方法面临两大挑战：活动仅以低级事件形式观测，且真实工作是多线程、目标交错的。已有方法要么假设给定单一任务或工作流，要么只产生步骤级摘要而非结构化任务模型，无法满足代理学习和组织审计的需求。

Method: TMI（Task Model Induction）包含两个核心步骤：(i) 在无约束的轨迹中发现潜在任务，将并发的多线程活动进行解耦；(ii) 对每个潜在任务归纳任务模型，该模型由两部分组成——层次化目标模型（递归目标分解）和程序模型（组织执行的控制流）。

Result: 在受控的人类与代理轨迹上，TMI恢复交错任务的分组一致率达到0.974，重建了74.9%的观察执行步骤，远超最强的工作流归纳基线。在外在评估中，基于TMI任务模型提取的技能在保留集上提升了30.0%的任务准确率。

Conclusion: TMI能够从无约束的自然主义交互轨迹中有效发现并结构化建模多线程任务，生成可审计且可复用的任务模型，其派生技能显著提升了下游任务性能，为计算机使用代理在实际工作中的学习和组织知识管理提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Naturalistic computer-use traces, passively recorded screenshots and mouse or keyboard actions, are a valuable resource for deriving symbolic, auditable, and reusable models of how everyday work is done. Such models matter as computer-use agents enter real work, where agents need to learn how tasks are actually performed, and organizations need to audit and reuse that knowledge. However, inducing such task models is challenging, as activity is observed only as low-level events and real-world work is multi-threaded with interleaved goals. Existing methods assume a given task or a single workflow, and produce step-level summaries rather than structured task models. We introduce Task Model Induction (TMI), which (i) discovers the latent tasks in an unconstrained trace, disentangling concurrent activity, and (ii) for each latent task, induces a task model pairing a hierarchical objective model of recursive goal decomposition with a procedure model of the control flow that organized the execution. Intrinsically, on controlled human and agent trajectories, TMI recovers interleaved tasks with 0.974 agreement against ground-truth groupings and reconstructs 74.9% of the observed execution steps, far more than the strongest workflow induction baseline. Extrinsically, skills derived from TMI's task models improve held-out task accuracy by 30.0% over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [48] [G-CARL: Grounded Checklist-Aligned Reward Learning for Patient-Oriented Medical Report Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20331)
*Shiao Xie,Siyu Chen,Jianwei Lv,Bo Yuan,Yujin Wang,Xiandong Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出患者导向的医学报告解读（PMRI）这一新型多模态生成任务，并设计G-CARL框架，通过多源检索与检查清单对齐的强化学习，在保证医学事实准确性的同时实现面向患者的可读性解读，构建了真实世界基准MMedReport并验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者对医学报告的个人化解读需求日益增长，这同时要求基于证据的医学事实准确性和依赖上下文的患者沟通能力。现有医学视觉-语言任务未能充分涵盖这两个核心要求，且二者在可验证性上存在根本差异却又紧密耦合，使得传统监督微调和整体强化学习范式难以联合优化。

Method: 提出G-CARL框架：（1）多源检索用于原子性（atomic）声明验证，提供事实性监督；（2）上下文感知的实例级加权检查清单用于回复覆盖度，同时覆盖用户需求满意度和表达质量；（3）基于检查清单对齐的强化学习提供结构化监督，且不限制回复多样性。同时构建MMedReport真实世界基准和临床医生设计的3D评估协议。

Result: 大量实验表明G-CARL在整体质量、声明级精度（claim-level precision）和检查清单召回率上持续优于现有后训练基线；临床医生的配对偏好评估进一步确认G-CARL生成的结果更准确且更符合患者需求。

Conclusion: G-CARL有效解决了医学报告个人化解读中事实准确性与患者沟通能力联合优化的难题，为医学视觉-语言模型的临床落地提供了可行的后训练方案。

Abstract: Personalized interpretation of medical reports has emerged as an increasingly important need among patients. Addressing this need requires both evidence-grounded medical factuality and context-dependent patient communication, yet existing medical vision-language tasks do not adequately capture these dual requirements. To bridge this gap, we introduce Patient-oriented Medical Report Interpretation (PMRI), a novel open-ended multimodal generation task that requires models to explain medical reports in accurate and accessible language based on a user's query and dialogue history. These two objectives differ fundamentally in their verifiability, yet remain tightly coupled, making them difficult to optimize jointly under conventional supervised fine-tuning and holistic reinforcement learning paradigms. To address this challenge, we propose G-CARL, a grounded, checklist-aligned reinforcement learning framework that combines multi-source retrieval for atomic claim verification with context-aware, instance-specific weighted checklists for response coverage, providing structured supervision for factuality, user-demand satisfaction, and expression quality without constraining response diversity. We further construct MMedReport, a real-world PMRI benchmark, along with a clinician-designed three-dimensional evaluation protocol. Extensive experiments demonstrate that G-CARL consistently outperforms existing post-training baselines in overall quality, claim-level precision, and checklist recall. Pairwise preference evaluation by clinicians further confirms that G-CARL produces interpretations that are more accurate and better aligned with patient needs.

</details>


### [49] [ConceptGuard: Benchmarking Context-Sensitive Unlearning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20338)
*Sahil Kale,Ian Harris*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ConceptGuard基准，将大语言模型（LLM）的"遗忘"（unlearning）问题提升到概念层面。与现有方法基于不相关事实的分离遗忘/保留集不同，ConceptGuard引入"双重用途概念"（dual-use concepts），即在有害和良性语境中都可使用的概念，通过互补的概念使用方式来构建遗忘和保留集，从而实现意图敏感的上下文分离评估。实验表明当前遗忘技术在概念层面的表现不佳，存在强遗忘-效用权衡、上下文敏感度提升有限、概念级控制一致性差等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法和基准存在根本性缺陷：它们依赖由独立事实组成的不相交遗忘/保留集，仅用简单直接的事实回忆来衡量成功与否。这种做法无法捕捉遗忘的真正需求——在消除有害行为的同时保留良性且有益的知识。有效遗忘必须在概念层面运作，确保完全移除不安全应用的同时维持其正确和有用的用法。

Method: 1) 提出"双重用途概念"（dual-use concepts）的概念定义——即可在有害和良性语境中使用的概念；2) 基于此构建ConceptGuard基准，其中遗忘集和保留集在概念使用上明确互补；3) 采用意图敏感的评估方式，以最大化上下文分离为目标，从ROUGE和概念级指标两方面进行评估。

Result: 实验表明现有遗忘技术在ConceptGuard设置下表现不佳：表现出弱上下文分离能力和较差的ROUGE及概念级指标得分。研究揭示了明显的遗忘-效用权衡、有限的上下文敏感性提升以及不同方法间概念级控制的一致性较差。

Conclusion: 当前遗忘方法未能满足现实世界安全要求中对概念级、意图敏感的遗忘需求。该研究为设计更符合实际安全需求的遗忘方法提供了思路，并公开了数据集以促进该方向的进一步研究。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly require selective removal of harmful or sensitive knowledge, called unlearning, yet existing methods and benchmarks fail to evaluate this capability completely. Current approaches rely on disjoint forget and retain sets composed of independent facts, and measure success using simple and direct factual recall. This framing fails to capture a key requirement of unlearning, namely the ability to eliminate harmful behaviors while preserving benign and beneficial knowledge. We argue that effective unlearning must operate at the level of concepts, ensuring complete removal of unsafe applications while maintaining their correct and useful usage, thereby achieving conceptually meaningful and complete unlearning. To better evaluate unlearning techniques from such a practical viewpoint, we introduce the notion of dual-use concepts: concepts that can be used in both harmful and benign contexts. Building on these concepts, we construct a benchmark called ConceptGuard where forget and retain sets are explicitly complementary in concept usage. Our benchmark uniquely enables unlearning to be explored and gauged at the level of concepts, instead of sparse facts, and evaluation is intent-sensitive with the goal of maximizing contextual separation to promote safer behavior. We demonstrate that current unlearning techniques perform poorly under this setting, showing weak contextual separation alongside poor performance in ROUGE and concept-level metrics. Our results reveal strong forgetting-utility trade-offs, limited gains in contextual sensitivity, and poor consistency in concept-level control across methods, and provide ideas for unlearning approaches that better align with real-world safety requirements. Our dataset is publicly available.

</details>

### [50] [Beyond linear subspaces: Nonlinear moment matching meets quadratic manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19486)
*Reetish Padhi,Serkan Gugercin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需优化的系统无关框架，用于直接构造二次投影矩阵进行模型降阶，该框架能匹配全阶系统的非线性矩并保留精确中心流形映射，在慢Kolmogorov n-宽度衰减的线性控制系统中实现高保真降维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性控制系统中慢Kolmogorov n-宽度衰减问题限制了线性子空间模型降阶的效果，二次流形方法虽能克服这一局限，但缺乏系统理论框架来构造此类二次逼近。

Method: 提出一种系统无关且免优化的框架，直接构造二次投影矩阵；通过理论证明所合成的降阶模型与全阶系统匹配非线性矩，并保留精确的中心流形映射。

Result: 在一维阻尼波方程和平流方程等输运主导基准问题上，该方法在高保真轨迹重建的同时显著降低状态空间维度，大幅节省在线计算量。

Conclusion: 该框架为二次流形模型降阶提供了首个系统理论支撑，在特定输入类型下保证了稳态输出的渐近跟踪，实现了降维精度与计算效率的良好平衡。

Abstract: Quadratic manifold-based model order reduction offers a viable pathway to circumvent the limitations of linear subspaces for linear control systems characterized by slow Kolmogorov $n$-width decay. However, a system-theoretic framework for constructing such quadratic approximations remains absent from the literature. This paper presents a system-agnostic, optimization-free framework for the direct construction of quadratic projection matrices. We prove that the synthesized reduced-order model matches the nonlinear moments of the full-order system and preserves its exact center manifold mapping, thereby ensuring asymptotic tracking of steady-state outputs under specific input classes. Numerical results on transport-dominated benchmark problems, namely, the one-dimensional damped wave and advection equations, show that the proposed framework achieves high-fidelity trajectory reconstruction within a significantly reduced-dimensional state space, yielding substantial online computational savings.

</details>


### [51] [On the Value Function of Infinite-Horizon Optimal Control of Piecewise Affine Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19530)
*Francesco Cordiano,Kanghui He,Bart De Schutter*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了分段仿射（PWA）系统在约束无限时间最优控制（CITOC）问题中值函数的结构，证明了在ℓ1或ℓ∞阶段代价下，值函数可以具有无限多个仿射片段（即非proper PWA函数），并给出了确保值函数为proper PWA函数（即紧集上有限个仿射片段）的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Existing works, such as [1], establish that the resulting value function is PWA in the state. However, existing results do not analyze whether the value function is a proper PWA function, i.e., with a finite number of affine pieces over compact sets, or whether the number of pieces can be infinite. We show that the latter case is indeed possible by means of an explicit example, which is also instrumental in establishing rigorous and easily verifiable sufficient conditions that ensure that the resulting value function is a proper PWA function. Our theoretical findings complement well-known results, e.g., the linear-quadratic case, and serve as support for recent learning-based control schemes for PWA systems. Throughout the paper, the proposed results are illustrated by means of a numerical example.

Abstract: Rather than prescribing an evaluation interval a priori, the proposed framework characterizes each evolving unsteady flow using a sequence of time windows. Abstract: High-multiplicity flexible job shops involve many copies of a small set of job types that must be scheduled on alternative machines. Fluid relaxations provide scalable workload lower bounds, but their fractional machine allocations do not define feasible schedules for individual jobs. We show that, after a suitable finite scaling, an optimal fluid allocation can be realized exactly by a feasible repeating discrete schedule. The construction scales the fluid allocation to integer operation counts, places the resulting operations in nonoverlapping machine intervals, repeats this arrangement, and links operations across repetitions into individual jobs without moving any interval, thereby enforcing job precedence while preserving machine feasibility. For growing finite instances with the same job-type composition, even with a fixed number of extra jobs, the gap between the optimal makespan and the fluid lower bound remains bounded by a constant; hence the relative gap vanishes as the instance grows. Guided by this repeated structure, we develop type-based cyclic template replay (TCTR), which searches job-type templates using an optimization model whose size does not grow with the number of job copies and replays the selected template on the full instance. On 676 multiplicity-expanded public flexible-job-shop instances, TCTR is feasible in every case and achieves a 1.54% mean gap to the fluid lower bound, compared with 5.32% for a job-indexed adaptive large-neighborhood search and 6.18% for a hybrid genetic algorithm.

</details>

Method: 通过理论分析，对给定的极值有限集，证明删除悬垂点时，基于该集合的Meissner多面体所能达到的最小表面积不会增加；反之添加悬垂点时，最小可达到的表面积不会减小。这两个结论相结合，将寻找面积最小Meissner多面体的问题归约为无悬垂点的生成集。

Result: 证明了删除悬垂点不会增加最小表面积，添加悬垂点不会减小最小表面积。因此面积最小的Meissner多面体必然可以在无悬垂点的生成集上找到。

Conclusion: 寻找面积最小化Meissner多面体的搜索空间可以限制在无悬垂点的生成集上，从而简化了该几何优化问题的求解。

Abstract: Meissner polyhedra are constant-width bodies obtained from extremal finite sets of unit diameter. Such a generating set may contain dangling points, namely points having exactly two diametric neighbors. This article studies whether these points can play an essential role in surface-area minimization. Given an extremal set we show that the smallest surface area among the Meissner polyhedra based on it cannot increase by deleting a dangling point. Adding a dangling point cannot decrease the smallest achievable surface area. This reduces the search for area-minimizing Meissner polyhedra to generating sets without dangling points.

</details>

Abstract: This paper proposes a controller design method for shaping the controllability Gramian into a desired form to design the effect from exogenous inputs to the system state. When the exogenous input is modeled as Gaussian white noise, the proposed framework is closely related to $H_2$ control, which can be interpreted as a special case of optimal transport. Numerical examples demonstrate anisotropic controllability design for a guidance robot and verify the ability to impose additional directional constraints through LMIs. The numerical examples also confirm that the proposed method approaches $H_2$ control as the desired Gramian tends to zero.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典增广拉格朗日方法在非线性等约束优化中有效，但其非线性原始子问题的求解代价高昂。现有线性化增广拉格朗日方法通常引入原始-对偶线性化误差，导致乘子更新不一致。作者旨在设计一种方法，在保留经典乘子更新（基于非线性残差）的同时，仅通过线性化原始模型计算步长，并严格刻画由此引入的约束线性化误差对乘子恒等式的影响。

Abstract: Augmented Lagrangian methods are effective for nonlinear equality-constrained optimization, but solving their nonlinear primal subproblems can be expensive. For smooth nonconvex problems with deterministic or stochastic objectives, we propose a nonlinear-residual linearized augmented Lagrangian method (NR-LALM) that replaces this subproblem by a regularized Gauss-Newton-type step while retaining the classical multiplier update based on the nonlinear constraint residual. The resulting step is computed from one symmetric positive-definite linear system, but the mismatch between the linearized primal model and the nonlinear-residual update produces a quadratic constraint-linearization error in the multiplier identity. We show that this error can be controlled under local regularity; multiplier boundedness and trajectory localization are derived rather than assumed. With fixed, accuracy-independent parameters, deterministic NR-LALM finds an $\varepsilon$-approximate Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) pair in $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ iterations and first-order oracle evaluations. For stochastic objectives, a projected stochastic path-integrated differential estimator with safeguarded restarts requires, in expectation, $O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ stochastic-gradient evaluations and $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ constraint and Jacobian evaluations. Compactness and a Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz condition further yield finite-length convergence of the deterministic primal-dual sequence. An optional minimum-norm second-order correction reduces the constraint-linearization error from second to fourth order without changing the complexity orders. All theoretical results are formalized in Lean 4. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted error orders and show favorable performance on high-dimensional deterministic and stochastic problems.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce a model with control vacations instead of standard queueing control systems with permanent control. The researched model is an M/M/1 queue with temporary periods of service rate control with two available service rates. After a control period of exponentially distributed length, a control vacation is initiated during which a fixed service rate $μ$ is used. The start of the next control period needs to be scheduled directly at a certain cost. We will use the Markov Decision Process from Kanavetas et al. arXiv:2605.31573 to find a sufficient condition that ensures that the average expected cost can be reduced w.r.t. the model that only uses the fixed service rate. Under this condition we will use properties of this related process to construct a cost reducing policy. The process with control vacations under specific policies induces a renewal reward process. We use a Tauberian theorem to relate the average expected cost of this renewal reward process to a vanishing discount method and analytically determine a lower bound of the average expected cost reduction. Finally, we study the actual attained average cost reduction for these policies through simulation.

Motivation: 带拖车汽车的前馈转向输入计算对n的规模高度敏感：标准的迭代符号微分方法随拖车数量n呈超指数增长，即使借助计算机代数软件，在n约5时计算量便已变得不可处理。因此需要一种能够有效扩展至更大拖车数量的实用计算方法。

Method: 提出一种结合四种关键成分的算法：(i) 引入角中间变量简化表达式结构；(ii) 将递归在子映射中进行因子化处理；(iii) 使用缩放的高阶乘积/商法则避免表达式膨胀；(iv) 采用基于截断形式幂级数的组合法则替代Faa di Bruno公式。算法整体具有O(r³)的算术复杂度（r为导数阶数）。

Result: 基准测试显示：SymPy直接符号微分在导数阶r=14时达到20秒超时；Bell多项式实现的Faa di Bruno公式在r=24后超过60秒超时；而所提方法在r=40以内均保持毫秒级计算（小于1毫秒），数值结果与参考方法在有效范围内达到机器精度一致。端到端演示成功计算并动画展示了20辆拖车的泊车机动。

Abstract: We address the practical computation of feedforward steering inputs for a car with n trailers via differential flatness. The standard route (iterated symbolic differentiation of the flat output) scales prohibitively with n: even with computer-algebra software the expression sizes grow super-exponentially and become intractable beyond n approx. 5. We propose an algorithm that combines four ingredients (angular intermediate variables, factorisation of the recursion in submaps, rescaled higher-order product/quotient rules, and a composition rule based on truncated formal power series instead of Faa di Bruno's formula). We benchmark the proposed method against (i) direct symbolic differentiation in SymPy and (ii) Faa di Bruno's formula evaluated via Bell polynomials. Both reference methods hit a clear computational wall: SymPy reaches a 20 s timeout at derivative order r = 14, while the Bell-polynomial recursion exceeds a 60 s timeout beyond r = 24. The proposed approach has O(r3) arithmetic complexity in the derivative order and remains under one millisecond up to r = 40, with numerical agreement to machine precision in the regime where the reference methods succeed. As an end-to-end illustration, a 20-trailer parking manoeuvre is computed and animated.

</details>

Abstract: The term "dual control" refers to the dual objective of simultaneously balancing exploration and exploitation. Problems of this kind have been studied for nearly a century. This paper is devoted to theory and methodology relevant for optimal control of linear time-invariant systems whose parameters are initially unknown and must be learned by active probing. We review the main ideas underlying four major research directions: Multi-armed bandits, self-tuning regulators, regret rate minimizing controllers, and minimax optimal dual controllers. The first three have a long history and rich literature, whereas the fourth provides a promising framework for robust dual control.

Result: 文章成功建立了从线性可控性到SO(n)等矩阵李群上非线性可控性的系统化李理论框架。SO(3)数值模拟展示了受控旋转的几何形态，SO(4)和SO(7)的显式对易子序列使局部控制方向的传播过程清晰可见。Yamabe步骤的详细证明和上三角矩阵代数的可解、幂零、理想例证完善了可达子群论证，并与Sussmann的局部可控性理论形成了系统对比。

Conclusion: 局部代数信息（李代数结构）确实可以在矩阵李群上传播为全局可达性结论，但这一传播需要谨慎处理无穷维李代数与有限维李对应理论失效的情形。旋转群提供了理想的具体实验场景，而可达子群论证结合Yamabe步骤构成了连接线性与非线性可控性的关键桥梁。

Abstract: This article develops a self-contained Lie-theoretic route from linear controllability to nonlinear controllability on matrix Lie groups. The organizing question is whether local algebraic information can be propagated into statements about reachable sets. The linear case supplies the model: the matrix exponential, Cayley-Hamilton theorem, trajectory formula and Kalman family B,AB,...,An-1B reduce controllability to finite-dimensional linear algebra. In the nonlinear setting, Lie brackets replace matrix powers, but the associated Lie algebra may be infinite dimensional and the finite-dimensional Lie correspondence can fail. Particular emphasis is placed on rotation groups. Skew-symmetric matrices, their exponentials, commutators, one-parameter subgroups, and the Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff formula provide a concrete laboratory for seeing how infinitesimal directions propagate on SO(n). A numerical SO(3) simulation illustrates the geometry of controlled rotations, while explicit SO(4) and SO(7) commutator sequences make the propagation of a localized control direction visible. The article then develops the attainable-subgroup argument for right-invariant systems, gives a detailed proof sequence behind the Yamabe step, and works through solvable, nilpotent and ideal examples for upper-triangular matrix algebras. The finite-dimensional mechanism is finally contrasted with Sussmann's local controllability theory for general nonlinear systems.

</details>

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of estimating upper bounds on the probability that a dynamical system will enter an undesirable region at some point within a finite time horizon. The primary source of uncertainty lies in the system's initial state, for which only a finite set of moments is known or within a prescribed interval. To tackle this problem, we formulate a measure-based program and propose its relaxation using the moment-sum-of-squares (moment-SOS) framework. The corresponding dual problem is introduced as a functional program, which is subsequently strengthened into a sum-of-squares (SOS) program. Notably, this dual formulation bears a structural resemblance to classical barrier function techniques for certifying system safety, with the key distinction that it yields a probabilistic certificate. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is demonstrated through multiple case studies, including a case involving an object in orbit.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端损失上的CVaR约束是实践中广泛使用的风险管理工具，但连续时间动态投资组合优化在CVaR约束下的理论分析和高效算法仍有不足。现有方法多依赖市场完备性或特定效用函数，且缺乏对最优策略结构特征的深入理解。

Method: 利用CVaR的辅助阈值表示建立对偶问题；在凸交易目标的一般框架下证明强对偶性和最优策略存在性；设计嵌套二分-黄金分割搜索算法，外层对阈值和Lagrange乘子搜索，内层迭代归结为标准无约束随机控制问题；理论证明算法收敛性，并通过数值实验（含不可交易禀赋风险和价格冲击）验证策略性质。

Result: 风险约束非绑定时恢复经典Merton策略；约束绑定时最优策略为状态依赖型——不利结果后降低风险敞口，有利结果后保持甚至临近到期时增加敞口；终端CVaR约束导致跨状态的非对称再配置而非均匀降风险；不可交易禀赋风险放大保守调整，价格冲击降低期望头寸和调整速度。

Conclusion: 终端CVaR约束下的动态最优策略具有状态依赖的非对称再配置特征，区别于传统均匀去风险直觉；所提出的对偶嵌套算法在一般凸目标下具备收敛保证，为实践中的CVaR约束组合管理提供了理论基础与高效计算工具。

Abstract: We study continuous-time dynamic portfolio optimization under a Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) constraint on the investor's terminal loss. For a general class of convex trading objectives, we exploit the auxiliary-threshold representation of CVaR to establish the existence of an optimal strategy and strong duality without requiring market completeness. These results motivate a dual-based nested bisection--golden-search algorithm over the threshold and Lagrangian multiplier, where the inner iterations reduce to standard unconstrained stochastic control problems. We prove that the resulting strategies converge to the optimal control as the number of iterations tends to infinity. Numerical experiments recover the Merton policy when the risk constraint is nonbinding. When the constraint is binding, the optimal strategy becomes state dependent: the investor reduces risky exposure following adverse outcomes but preserves, and near maturity may increase, exposure following favorable outcomes. Thus, a terminal CVaR constraint produces an asymmetric reallocation across states rather than uniform de-risking. Nontraded endowment risk amplifies the conservative adjustment, whereas price impact lowers desired positions and adjustment speeds.

</details>


### [63] [Direct vs. Indirect Data-Driven Control: Case-study of Switching Systems Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20207)

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于间接方法的切换线性系统稳定性分析框架，从含噪声状态测量数据中推断系统模型，再结合二次李雅普诺夫分析与机器学习泛化界、系统辨识灵敏度分析，提供有限样本收敛率保证。同时扩展了直接数据驱动方法以处理测量噪声，并通过数值实验表明在中等至高噪声水平下，间接方法比直接方法具有更紧的概率保证和更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动控制中直接方法与间接方法是两种主要范式：直接方法已发展出切换线性系统稳定性分析的有限样本保证，但间接方法在这方面的保证仍大部分缺失，亟需填补这一理论空白。

Method: 采用间接方法，先从含噪声的状态测量数据中辨识系统模型，然后结合三方面技术：(1)机器学习的泛化界、(2)系统辨识方法、(3)二次李雅普诺夫分析的灵敏度分析，共同推导出系统收敛率的有限样本保证。同时扩展了现有的直接数据驱动方法，使其能够处理有界测量噪声之外更一般的噪声情形。

Result: 数值实验表明，在中等至高噪声水平下，间接方法相比直接方法能提供更紧的概率保证，并且对噪声和离群值具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 间接方法在含噪声场景下具有理论保证上的优势，为数据驱动的切换线性系统稳定性分析提供了一种新的、具有有限样本保证的理论框架。

Abstract: A central methodological question in data-driven control is whether to adopt a direct or indirect approach. Direct methods infer a controller or certificate directly from data, while indirect methods first identify a system model and then apply model-based control techniques. Recent developments of the direct method have led to finite-sample guarantees for the data-driven stability analysis of switched linear systems under various settings. However, for the indirect method, such guarantees remain largely elusive. In this paper, we provide a novel framework for the stability analysis of switched linear systems from noisy state measurements, using the indirect approach and quadratic Lyapunov analysis. Our framework comes with finite-sample guarantees on the convergence rate of the system. For that, we combine generalization bounds from machine learning and system identification with sensitivity analysis from quadratic Lyapunov analysis. To enable comparison, we also extend existing direct data-driven methods to handle measurement noise beyond the bounded noise case currently available in the literature. Finally, we compare the two approaches through numerical experiments, revealing that under moderate-to-high noise levels the indirect approach yields tighter probabilistic guarantees as well as greater robustness to noise and outliers than the direct approach

</details>

*Weihao Mao,Jane J. Ye*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有值函数方向次微分的结果主要集中在有限维空间，而无限维空间的敏感性分析需要更一般的理论框架。无限维空间中紧致性的缺失使得传统方法失效，亟需新的方向性条件来填补这一空白。

Method: 在Asplund空间框架下，建立方向性次微分的演算规则，将有限维方向的非光滑分析推广至无限维；引入新的方向性条件来替代或弥补有界集紧致性的缺失，并基于此推导值函数方向极限次微分与奇异次微分的上界估计。

Result: 在Asplund空间中建立了方向性次微分的关键演算规则，提出了能够应对无限维非紧致性挑战的新方向性条件，并成功推导出值函数方向极限次微分和奇异次微分的上界估计。

Conclusion: 该工作为无限维层次系统的敏感性分析提供了更精细的变分分析理论基础，拓展了方向非光滑分析在无限维空间中的适用性，是敏感性分析理论的重要进展。

Abstract: Directional subdifferentials of the value function provide a quantitative measure of optimal value response to perturbations. While existing results are largely limited to finite-dimensional settings, this paper develops a comprehensive variational framework in Asplund spaces. We establish essential directional calculus rules, extending directional nonsmooth analysis to infinite dimensions. To address the lack of compactness of bounded sets in infinite-dimensional spaces, we introduce a new directional condition, under which we derive upper estimates for directional limiting and singular subdifferentials of the value function. These results provide a refined analytical foundation for sensitivity analysis in infinite-dimensional hierarchical systems.

</details>

### [65] [Active Inference as Context Acquisition for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19202)
*Sanchayan Dutta,Sai Niranjan Ramachandran,Suvrit Sra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将交互式AI代理的上下文获取问题形式化为主动推断（active inference）框架，通过内部推断步骤更新潜在任务状态的信念，外部决策选择下一步动作以在令牌成本下最小化期望自由能，并实例化为最优提问(OQA)框架，在基准测试中验证了其在分类任务、澄清生成和提示优化上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式AI代理需要在用户省略约束、偏好、文件或任务变量时，尽快获取正确的上下文。但代理可以选择默认假设（节省成本但可能出错）或花费令牌进行澄清、检索等操作。如何在效率与正确性之间做出最优权衡成为一个关键问题，目前缺乏统一的数学框架来指导这一决策。

Method: 将上下文获取建模为主动推断问题：内部推断步骤更新潜在任务状态上的信念（后验），外部决策在下一步上下文动作、任务动作或停止动作之间选择，以最小化期望自由能（含成本项）。确定性设置下认识论项简化为期望信息增益（可选用令牌成本归一化）。实例化为最优提问（OQA）框架，采用精确后验和动态规划oracle，并在25到300个候选的二元和多分类任务上基准测试前沿语言模型。

Result: 在二元和多路分类任务（25至300个候选）上对前沿语言模型进行了基准测试，并研究了在令牌预算下澄清生成与自动提示优化。OQA框架在这些任务上取得了优于基线的方法效果。

Conclusion: 该框架是模型无关的，将主动推断视为AI代理上下文获取层的设计原则，为构建高效交互式代理提供了理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: Interactive AI agents must acquire the right context as efficiently as possible. When a user omits a constraint, preference, file, or task variable, an agent can proceed with a default assumption or spend tokens on a clarifying question, retrieval call, tool call, or prompt trial. We formulate this tradeoff as active inference for context acquisition. An inner inference step updates beliefs over a latent task state, and an outer decision selects the next context action, task action, or stop action to minimize expected free energy under cost. In deterministic settings, the epistemic term reduces to expected information gain, optionally normalized by token cost. We instantiate the framework in Optimal Question Asking (OQA), with exact posteriors and a dynamic programming oracle, and benchmark frontier language models on binary and multiway categorical tasks from 25 to 300 candidates. We also study clarification before generation and automated prompt optimization under token budgets. The formulation is model-agnostic and views active inference as a design principle for the context-acquisition layer of AI agents.

</details>


### [66] [Robust Metaheuristics under Uncertainty for Berth Allocation and Quay Crane Assignment: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19214)
*Yang Li,Peilan Xu,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是第一篇专门针对不确定性条件下BACAP（泊位分配与岸桥调度）问题鲁棒种群元启发式优化的综述性文章，系统梳理了不确定性来源、信息表示、机制导向的方法分类（解表示与解码、鲁棒评估与选择、鲁棒引导的搜索动力学、可行性保持与恢复），并提供了基准测试套件与基线结果，最后指出开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BACAP是港口码头调度中的代表性组合优化问题，在实际执行中面临到达偏差、装卸时间波动和资源中断等不确定性，名义假设下优化的调度方案可能脆弱。尽管种群元启发式方法已被广泛用于BACAP及其相关问题，但现有研究在不确定性表示、鲁棒性准则、搜索机制和实验评估协议方面较为碎片化，缺乏系统性的综述总结。

Method: 作者首先总结了BACAP中的不确定性来源与信息表示方式；其次从机制导向角度对现有鲁棒种群元启发式方法进行分类，涵盖四个维度：解表示与解码、鲁棒评估与选择、鲁棒性引导的搜索动力学、以及可行性保持与恢复；最后构建了一个面向不确定BACAP的基准测试套件，并通过组合代表性元启发式与不同鲁棒策略报告了说明性基线结果。

Result: 本文提供了第一个专注于不确定条件下BACAP鲁棒种群元启发式的系统综述，包括不确定性建模的分类学、四种机制维度的方法组织框架、一个支持受控实验比较的基准套件，以及代表性组合策略下的基线实验结果。

Conclusion: 作者识别出四项开放挑战：基准扩展、鲁棒性感知的搜索设计、时间自适应鲁棒性以及非平稳不确定性处理，为未来研究方向提供了指引。

Abstract: The berth allocation and quay crane assignment problem (BACAP) is a representative port-terminal scheduling problem in maritime transportation and freight logistics, where vessel arrivals, berth positions, service durations, and quay?crane availability are tightly coupled. Under uncertainties such as arrival deviations, handling-time fluctuations, and resource disruptions, schedules optimized under nominal assumptions may become fragile during execution, motivating the study of robust metaheuristic optimization for BACAP in port-terminal operations. Although population-based metaheuristics have been widely used for BACAP and related port-scheduling problems, existing studies remain fragmented in their uncertainty repre?sentations, robustness criteria, search mechanisms, and empir?ical evaluation protocols. To the best of our knowledge, this paper provides the first focused review dedicated to robust population-based metaheuristics for BACAP under uncertainty. We first summarize uncertainty sources and information repre?sentations in BACAP, and then organize existing methods from a mechanism-oriented perspective, covering solution representation and decoding, robust evaluation and selection, robustness-guided search dynamics, and feasibility preservation and recovery. We further present a benchmark suite for uncertain BACAP to support controlled empirical comparison and report illustrative baseline results by combining representative metaheuristics with different robustness strategies. Finally, we identify open chal?lenges related to benchmark extension, robustness-aware search design, time-adaptive robustness, and non-stationary uncertainty.

</details>


### [67] [How to Navigate Uncertainty About AI Consciousness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19215)
*Dr Tom McClelland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将关于AI意识的问题从不可解的"是否拥有意识"转向可解的"是否拥有效价状态"，以此为基础确立对潜在有知觉AI的负责任开发方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在关于人工意识存在深刻不确定性的背景下，我们面临两难困境：假设AI无感觉可能对值得道德关怀的实体造成严重伤害，假设AI有感觉则可能浪费资源在无感觉的机器上。由于AI意识问题的不可解性，这一困境难以摆脱。

Method: 作者提出从不可解的AI意识问题转向可解的AI效价（valence）问题：评估AI是否具有那些若其有意识便会构成效价体验的状态，并以这种评估为基础建立对潜在有知觉AI的负责任研究方法。

Result: 作者展示了这种以效价为中心的评估框架足以支撑一种负责任的方法来处理潜在有意识的AI发展。

Conclusion: 通过将焦点从意识问题转向效价问题，可以为AI伦理决策提供更实际、可操作的基础，从而摆脱两难困境。

Abstract: Given deep uncertainty about the possibility of artificial consciousness, it is unclear how we should treat potentially sentient AI. On the one hand, we could assume insentience but risk doing terrible harms to entities that deserve moral standing. On the other hand, we could assume sentience and instead risk wasting resources on insentient machines. The intractability of questions around AI consciousness mean that this dilemma is hard to escape. I suggest a way out of that shifts from intractable questions of AI consciousness to tractable questions of AI valence. Specifically, we can assess whether an AI has states that would constitute valenced experiences if it were conscious. I show how this is sufficient to ground a responsible approach to the development of potentially conscious AI.

</details>


### [68] [Bounded Sovereignty and the Control Tax: Pricing AI Oversight When the Deployer Does Not Own the Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19216)
*Zhen Wen Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"有界主权"（bounded sovereignty）概念，分析受监管组织通过API或托管端点使用前沿模型时面临的部分技术与合同访问权限制。论文构建了四层访问类型学、逐层协议需求矩阵和"主权折扣成本"概念，并通过135万例合成案例的访问消融实验及匿名国家支付基础设施场景进行阐释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI控制研究大多假设部署者能够对模型及其管线进行完全可观测和可干预，但这在受监管组织通过API使用前沿模型时往往不成立——部署者可能只能控制业务流程，无法控制模型权重、服务基础设施、内部追踪、更新过程或完整交互日志。这种访问能力错配决定了哪些控制协议在实际中可执行。

Method: 提出四层访问类型学（数据、模型、基础设施、交互层），构建协议-逐层需求矩阵，定义主权折扣成本概念；基于135万合成案例进行访问消融实验，并通过匿名国家支付基础设施场景进行构念效度验证。

Result: 完整日志改善诊断能力，预执行网关支持干预，追踪访问和模型版本控制增强事后解释力，范围限制能提升安全性但降低实用性。

Conclusion: 通用控制协议应明确声明其访问假设；不同访问权限组合决定哪些控制协议在实践中可执行，受监管组织的控制税中相当部分用于替代缺失的访问权。

Abstract: AI control research asks how to deploy models safely even when they may be misaligned, but many control protocols assume that the deployer can instrument the model and its surrounding pipeline. That assumption often fails for regulated organisations using frontier models through APIs or managed endpoints, where the deployer may control the business process but not the model weights, serving infrastructure, internal traces, update process, or full interaction logs. This paper introduces bounded sovereignty: partial technical and contractual access across the data, model, infrastructure, and interaction layers of the AI stack. It argues that these access conditions determine which control protocols can be executed in practice. The paper contributes a four-layer access typology, a protocol-by-layer requirements matrix, and the concept of sovereignty discount cost: the part of the control tax spent substituting for missing access through contracts, architecture, audit, vendor assurance, residual risk, or reduced system scope. It also reports a synthetic access-ablation experiment over 1.35 million synthetic case simulations and interprets the findings through an anonymised national-payments-infrastructure scenario. The experiment is not real-world payment-system evidence; it is a construct-validity exercise. The results show that complete logs improve diagnosis, a pre-execution gateway enables intervention, trace access and model-version control strengthen post-incident explanation, and scope restriction can improve safety while reducing usefulness. Control protocols proposed as general safety solutions should therefore state their access assumptions explicitly.

</details>


### [69] [Interaction valence reveals contrasting social networks in dairy cattle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19222)
*Sibi Parivendan,Suresh Raja Neethirajan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于姿态估计的、带情感效价（valence-aware）的社交网络分析框架，用于将视频中奶牛互动自动转化为畜群层面的亲和与对抗组织表征，并在商业奶牛的挤奶前区域进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动化牲畜监测通常将行为视为孤立事件，忽略了社会关系对资源获取、冲突暴露和群体稳定性的影响。作者希望将视频中的互动提升到群体社交网络层面，并区分亲和性与对抗性关系，以更全面理解畜群社会结构。

Method: 采用基于姿态的计算机视觉流程，分析单个商业奶牛场挤奶前区域7小时39分钟连续视频。经质量控制保留1,183个候选互动（涉及36头牛、177个二元组）。通过预测类别平衡审核评估管道性能（自动与人工标注一致率82.8%，未加权审核样本宏F1为0.872），并构建聚合网络、计算密度、传递性、平均路径长度、模块度等指标，分离亲和性与对抗性网络层进行对比分析。

Result: 聚合网络连通（密度=0.281，传递性=0.513，平均路径长度=1.88）；预测亲和事件形成5个算法社区（模块度Q=0.429）。对抗性互动占保留事件的72.4%和互动时长的76.0%。互动伙伴最多的奶牛并非介数中心性最高者。按效价分离后，亲和层与对抗层在边集、社区划分和个体位置上呈现显著不同的描述性特征。

Conclusion: 合并统计互动次数会掩盖观察网络的互动行为组成；效价感知分析为检验竞争、亲和及福利相关变化的假设提供了框架，但作为福利或健康指标的常规使用前仍需纵向验证。

Abstract: Social relationships shape access to resources, exposure to conflict and group stability, yet automated livestock monitoring typically treats behaviour as isolated events. Here, we present a valence-aware social-network framework that transforms video-derived interactions into herd-level representations of affiliative and agonistic organization. A pose-based computer-vision pipeline analysed 7 h 39 min of continuous video from the pre-milking area of one commercial dairy farm. After quality control, 1,183 of 1,414 candidate interactions remained, involving 36 cows and 177 dyads. In a predicted-class-balanced audit of 198 pipeline-detected clips, automated and manual labels agreed in 82.8% of cases, with an unweighted audit-sample macro-F1 of 0.872. These values describe the audited sample rather than prevalence-weighted or end-to-end deployment performance. The aggregated network was connected (density = 0.281; transitivity = 0.513; mean path length = 1.88), and predicted affiliative events formed five algorithmic communities (modularity Q = 0.429). Within the observed zone, predicted agonistic interactions comprised 72.4% of retained events and 76.0% of interaction duration. The cow with the most partners did not have the highest betweenness centrality. Separating events by predicted valence produced descriptively different affiliative and agonistic layers, with contrasting edge sets, community partitions and individual positions. Thus, pooled interaction counts can obscure the behavioural composition of an observed network. Valence-aware analysis provides a framework for testing hypotheses about competition, affiliation and welfare-relevant change, while requiring longitudinal validation before use as a welfare or health indicator.

</details>


### [70] [Air Traffic Control Using Large Language Models: Prompt Engineering, Architecture, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19299)
*Mahyar Ghazanfari,Matthias Casanova,Jordan Kam,Alex Zongo,Peng Wei,Torsten Darrell,Alexandre Bayen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文实验评估大语言模型（LLM）能否生成运营上逼真的空中交通管制（ATC）通信。以旧金山"Bay Tour"航线上的一次实验性通用航空飞行的真实转录为基准，通过人机迭代设计五种约束递增的提示结构，嵌入有状态多轮管线中。测试九个开源和闭源LLM，变化提示、上下文示例及历史条件方式。结果显示：提供工作示例能提升相似度，但收紧提示反而有害，最严格脚本化提示因自身错误累积而崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ATC通信是安全关键的对话场景，且在空管其他环节已半自动化的背景下仍主要由人类驱动。本文希望探索LLM是否能够生成运营上逼真的ATC通话，为未来LLM辅助ATC指明可行路径及当前局限。

Method: 以真实转录的手工转写作为地面真值（P0），通过飞行员在环过程设计五种约束递增的提示结构（P1-P5），嵌入有状态多轮管线中（模型扮演ATC，配合固定飞行员转录，并依赖累积对话历史）。在九个开源和闭源LLM上，变化提示结构、是否提供不同实验航班的工作转录作为上下文示例、以及模型是基于自身先前回复还是注入地面真值历史进行条件化。使用词法、结构和语义相似度指标以及经人类专家标注验证的LLM-as-judge（GPT-5.5）进行评分。

Result: 提供工作示例总体上提高了相似度分数；而收紧提示并没有带来改进——最轻量级的提示效果最佳，最重度脚本化的提示因其自身错误在多轮对话中累积导致性能崩溃，而注入正确历史可以修复这种现象。

Conclusion: 这些结果勾勒出一条具体可行的路径，同时也揭示了当前LLM辅助ATC的局限所在。

Abstract: Air traffic control (ATC) communication is a safety-critical dialogue that remains largely human-driven even as other parts of air traffic management have been semi-automated. In this article, we experimentally evaluate whether large language models (LLMs) can generate operationally realistic ATC transmissions. An experimental general-aviation flight flying over the San Francisco "Bay Tour" route is hand-transcribed and used as ground truth (P0). Through a pilot-in-the-loop process we design five prompt structures (P1-P5) of increasing constraint and embed them in a stateful multi-turn pipeline, where the model plays ATC to a fixed pilot transcript while conditioning on the accumulating dialogue history. Across nine open- and closed-source LLMs we vary the prompt, the presence of a worked transcript from a different experimental flight as an in-context example, and whether the model conditions on its own prior replies or on injected ground-truth history. Turns are scored with lexical, structural, and semantic similarity metrics and by an LLM-as-judge (GPT-5.5) validated against human expert annotation. Supplying a worked example improves similarity, but tightening the prompt does not: the lightest prompts perform best and the most heavily scripted one collapses as its own errors accumulate through the dialogue, which injecting correct history repairs. These results outline a concrete path and its current limits toward LLM-assisted ATC.

</details>


### [71] [Outcome Monitors: Recovery Affordances for Silent Tool Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19303)
*Sugam Panthi,Rabab Abdelfattah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Outcome Monitors（结果监控器），用于检测工具调用结果中违反"结果契约"的错误（如缓存错误页、负价格等），并在检测到违规时保留结果、发出非约束性收据（包含违规属性和恢复工具）。在预设评估中，该方法将ToolMaze完成率从10.9%提升至28.1%，并在tau-bench零售场景中分别提升14.0和12.0个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具调用超时是明显可见的失败，代理可以绕行；但缓存错误页或负价格这类错误会以预期格式返回，被代理当作事实消费，难以被察觉。现有方法缺乏对这类"格式正确但语义错误"的结果的检测手段。

Method: 1) 从任务无关的轨迹中挖掘或从公开schema推导"结果契约"（outcome contracts）；2) 构建Outcome Monitors检测结果对契约的违反；3) 发生违规时保留结果，同时发布非约束性收据，收据中命名被违反的属性及公开恢复工具（recovery tools）。

Result: 1) 在冻结的预设评估中，ToolMaze完成率从10.9%提升至28.1%（覆盖四个模型、两个提供商家族，并在第三个中复现）；2) tau-bench零售两个档位分别提升14.0和12.0个百分点；3) 消融实验表明：移除恢复工具列表会消除效果，恢复后效果恢复；诊断细节与时机无显著影响；4) 效果集中在故障阻塞完成的情况；5) 在转录自公开事件分类学的测试集上，检测率降至46%，但交付照常、完成率不变。

Conclusion: 恢复工具是收据中的关键有效内容，而非诊断细节或时机；当前检测能力局限于已挖掘的契约词汇表，扩展检测范围到契约词汇表之外仍是开放问题。

Abstract: When a tool call times out, the agent sees the failure and can route around it. A cached error page or negative price can instead arrive in the expected format and be consumed as fact. We introduce Outcome Monitors, which detect violations of outcome contracts mined from task-disjoint traces or derived from public schemas. On a violation, the monitor preserves the result and issues a nonbinding receipt naming the violated property and public recovery tools. In frozen, prespecified evaluations with injected failures, Outcome Monitors raise ToolMaze completion from 10.9% to 28.1% across four models in two provider families and replicate in a third. In tau-bench retail, completion improves by 14.0 and 12.0 points on two tiers. In separate ToolMaze controls, removing the recovery-tool list eliminates the measured gain and restoring it recovers the effect; diagnostic detail and timing produce no detectable differences. Gains concentrate where the fault blocks completion. On a suite transcribed from a published incident taxonomy, detection outside the mined vocabulary falls to 46%, though delivery continues and completion is unchanged. Recovery tools are the active receipt content in these controls; extending detection beyond the contract vocabulary remains open.

</details>


### [72] [Beyond Imitation: Filtering On-Policy Distillation by Reasoning Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19408)
*Chen Yang,Haiyuan Wan,Rengrong Xiong,Yize Chen,Danny H. K. Tsang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出R2-OPD方法，通过构建教师奖励与独立推理进度奖励的双重轨迹内排序，在不一致时抑制蒸馏奖励，解决教师奖励与真实推理进程冲突的问题，提升语言模型推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy distillation (OPD)假设教师奖励是推理进展的合适代理，但在实践中这一假设不成立——推理步骤虽有明显进展，却可能因偏离教师输出而获得较低蒸馏奖励。这种教师奖励与真实推理进展的冲突需要被解决。

Method: R2-OPD构建两条轨迹内推理片段（reasoning span）排名：一条来自教师奖励，另一条来自独立估计的进展奖励。当两条排名不一致时，选择性抑制蒸馏奖励，以减少与推理进展相冲突的监督信号，同时保留有效的教师指导。

Result: 在标准OPD基础上一致提升，尤其在推理性能方面表现显著。

Conclusion: 通过奖励过滤机制调解教师奖励与真实推理进展之间的矛盾，可以更有效地进行策略蒸馏，提升模型推理能力。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as an effective framework for post-training language models by pairing student-generated trajectories with dense token-level supervision from a teacher. However, OPD implicitly assumes that teacher-derived rewards are an appropriate proxy for reasoning progress, and therefore treats all teacher feedback equally during policy optimization. While in practice, this assumption does not always hold. We observe that teacher-derived rewards often conflict with genuine reasoning progress, as reasoning steps with clear reasoning advancement may still receive lower distillation rewards, simply due to deviation from teacher's outputs. To address this mismatch, we propose Reasoning-Progress-Aware Reward Filtering for On-Policy Distillation (R2-OPD), which constructs two within-trajectory rankings of reasoning spans, one from teacher-derived rewards and the other from independently estimated progress reward. Distillation rewards are selectively suppressed whenever the two rankings disagree, reducing supervision that conflicts with reasoning progress while preserving effective teacher guidance. Our approach shows consistent improvement over standard OPD especially regarding reasoning performances.

</details>


### [73] [Symposium: Trust via Auditable Records for Communities of AI Scientist Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19511)
*Dexter Pratt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Symposium是一个正式框架和实用实现，用于记录小型科学研究社区中AI代理的操作，提供长期、不可变的代理驱动研究活动历史，包括分析、假设、数据和科学讨论，并支持基于目的的可信度评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在快速发展的AI研究环境中，小型科学社区使用多样的AI系统，需要一种方式将社区持久的历史记录与动态变化的代理系统分离，从而保留科学证据以供审计和信任评估。

Method: Symposium通过一个独立的发布基础设施来捕获科学论证，包括结构化声明、细粒度证据引用、假设和显式的证据使用声明，并提供代理提示组件和文档以支持快速部署。

Result: 提供了一个可工作的实现，包括发布基础设施、代理提示组件和文档，使社区能够快速建立和运行自己的Symposium社区。

Conclusion: Symposium区别于AI联合科学家代理或集成AI研究环境，它是一个将科学社区的持久历史与操作该历史的代理和其他系统分离的框架，为小型科学社区的AI驱动研究提供可审计的记录和信任基础。

Abstract: Symposium is a formal framework and practical implementation to record the operation of AI agents deployed by small scientific research communities. Symposium provides long-term, immutable histories of agent-driven research activity, leaving auditable trails of analyses, hypotheses, data, and scientific discourse. This shared record of published artifacts enables agents to build on prior work and preserves the evidence researchers and agents need to make purpose-dependent trust assessments. Symposium captures scientific argument, including structured claims, fine-grained evidence citations, assumptions, and explicit declarations of what material may and may not be used as evidence. Symposium differs from AI co-scientist agents or integrated AI research environments; it is a framework that separates a scientific community's durable history from the agents and other systems that operate on that history. It assumes that a community will use diverse AI systems in a rapidly evolving environment. A working implementation of the publication infrastructure, agent prompt components, and documentation are provided to enable users to rapidly set up and run their own Symposium community.

</details>


### [74] [From Retrieved Context to Runtime Control: Adaptive Compression for Edge-based RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19535)
*Zlatan Feric,Amir Taherin,Yanzhi Wang,David Kaeli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出在边缘设备上的RAG系统中采用遥测感知的自适应压缩策略。通过在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor上用Llama和Qwen生成器、Natural Questions和HotpotQA数据集及LLMLingua-2压缩器进行实验表征，发现生成阶段主导RAG预算（占延迟约90%、GPU能耗91%），中间压缩率可在质量损失极小的情况下降低GPU能耗高达53.2%、SoC能耗48.2%。作者主张运行时策略应结合工作负载特征和边缘遥测数据动态管理压缩率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统在边缘设备上部署时，检索上下文会显著增加提示词长度，导致prefill开销、KV缓存占用和能耗增加。虽然上下文压缩可以缓解这一问题，但现有方法通常采用固定压缩预算或离线选定压缩率，忽略了工作负载变化和边缘设备的实时状态。同时，压缩本身在边缘SoC上运行也有成本，可能抵消生成阶段的节省。因此需要动态、遥测感知的自适应压缩策略。

Method: 作者在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor边缘SoC上进行系统性实验表征：使用Llama和Qwen两种规模（7B-8B）的生成模型、Natural Questions和HotpotQA数据集、以及LLMLingua-2压缩器，量化RAG各阶段（检索、压缩、生成）的延迟和能耗占比，并探索不同压缩率对推理质量和能耗的影响，以识别自适应操作区间。

Result: 实验表明：对于7B-8B大型生成模型，生成阶段占单次查询延迟约90%、GPU能耗约91%；适度压缩可能错过节能机会，过度压缩则会损害推理质量；中间压缩率可在质量损失可忽略的情况下将GPU能耗降低高达53.2%、SoC总能耗降低48.2%。这验证了存在自适应操作区间，且压缩率选择需要结合工作负载特征与边缘遥测信息动态决策。

Conclusion: 作者提出边缘RAG系统应实现遥测信息引导的运行时自适应压缩策略，动态调整压缩预算以平衡压缩开销与生成节省。该工作为边缘AI系统设计提供了实验基础，未来方向包括开发基于遥测和负载特征的在线压缩率调节机制。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves language-model responses by grounding generation in external passages, which comes with overhead: retrieved context lengthens the prompt, increasing prefill work, KV-cache footprint, memory traffic, latency, and energy. Context compression offers a natural remedy by pruning retrieved text before generation. However, state-of-the-art context-compression methods are typically used with a fixed compression budget, or with the rate selected offline and then applied at inference time. This static view ignores both workload variation and the live state of the edge device. On an edge SoC, compression is not free: the compressor itself runs on the same SoC and consumes latency and energy that can offset any generation savings.
  This paper proposes a vision for telemetry-informed adaptive compression in edge RAG, grounded in experimental evidence. We characterize the compression tradeoff on the NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor using Llama and Qwen generators, Natural Questions and HotpotQA datasets, and LLMLingua-2 compression. Our measurements show that generation dominates the RAG budget for larger models, reaching roughly 90% of per-query latency and 91% of GPU energy for 7B-8B generators. Exploring the impact of the compression rate reveals an adaptive operating region: mild compression can miss energy opportunities, and overly aggressive compression can hurt inference quality. Intermediate compression can reduce GPU energy by up to 53.2%, and SoC energy by up to 48.2%, with negligible quality loss. We argue for runtime policies that dynamically manage compression, guided by workload features and edge telemetry.

</details>


### [75] [Enforcing LLM Safety through DMD-based Classification of Prompt-Response Embedding Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19579)
*Mohamed Akrout,Olivera Kotevska,Dan Wilson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文扩展了一个用于幻觉检测的动力学系统框架（Koopman算子）到LLM安全分类任务。通过将提示和响应嵌入高维空间，分别为安全和不安全状态拟合Koopman预测模型，并使用差分残差分数进行分类。实验表明，结合提示嵌入对交互依赖型违规检测有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在高风险场景中部署日益增多，但生成有毒、有害或违反政策内容的风险显著。如何在黑盒条件下高效检测这些不安全输出仍然是一个未解决的挑战。

Method: 1) 将提示和响应投影到高维嵌入空间；2) 分别为安全和不安全区域拟合基于Koopman的预测模型；3) 提出新的差分残差分数（比较安全和不安全区域的预测误差）来分类新输出；4) 关键创新是同时纳入提示和响应的嵌入动态，拟合Koopman算子以捕获两者的交互模式。

Result: 在三个安全基准上使用三个嵌入模型进行黑盒评估。结果显示：结合提示嵌入带来一致改进，尤其在配因果解码器（如Llama-3）时对交互依赖型违规检测效果显著；而仅响应型违规则更受益于稠密语义嵌入表示。

Conclusion: 该工作开辟了使用动力学系统分析AI系统的方向，而非主流的用AI建模动力学系统范式。Koopman框架在LLM安全分类中展现出前景，对交互依赖型违规尤其有效。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes applications, yet their tendency to generate toxic, harmful, or policy-violating content poses significant risks. Detecting these unsafe outputs efficiently in a black-box manner remains an open challenge. In this paper, we extend a recently proposed dynamical systems framework designed for hallucination detection to LLM safety classification. By projecting both prompts and responses into high-dimensional embedding spaces and fitting separate Koopman-based predictive models for safe and unsafe regimes, we classify new outputs using a new differential residual score that compares prediction errors of the safe and unsafe regimes. A key contribution is the incorporation of the prompt and response embedding dynamics, yielding fitted Koopman operators that capture crucial interaction patterns. We evaluate our black-box method across three safety benchmarks using three embedding models. Our results show that incorporating prompt embeddings yields consistent improvements, particularly for interaction-dependent violations when paired with causal decoders (e.g., in Llama-3), while response-only violations benefit more from dense semantic embedding representations. These findings opens the door for using dynamical systems to analyze AI systems rather than the dominant paradigm of using AI to model dynamical systems.

</details>


### [76] [Scientific Data Skills: Enabling Agent-Ready Scientific Data Services at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19625)
*Xiaohan Huang,Qingqing Long,Xiaolei Du,Siyu Pu,Jiawen Xu,Haotian Chen,Chenyang Zhao,Jinbiao Liu,Xuezhi Wang,Hao Wang,Hengshu Zhu,Yuanchun Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出科学数据技能（SciDSK），一种面向AI代理的数据集即服务表示方法，将数据集特定知识与操作指南打包为可复用代理技能，并通过科学数据技能银行平台在六个学科中发布，实验表明其能显著提升代理驱动的数据集发现与解释能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据日益被AI代理使用，但现有数据集表示对自主发现、解释和调用支持有限，主要原因在于科学数据分散在异构仓库中，且数据集表示主要面向人类使用而非机器代理。

Method: 设计了结构化SciDSK规范，整合数据集描述、科学背景、文件组织结构、使用流程、质量检查和溯源信息，同时保留底层数据在原仓库中；开发了基于权威数据集记录及相关支撑材料的系统化构建管线；建立了跨六个科学学科的科学数据技能银行平台，支持包访问、持久标识和溯源追踪。

Result: 通过数据集发现检索基准和受控数据集解释案例的评估，SciDSK显著改善了代理驱动的数据集发现，并为数据集解释提供了更精确、更可操作的支持。

Conclusion: 研究结果验证了以代理就绪表示形式组织数据集特定知识的价值，说明SciDSK是弥合异构科学数据与AI代理之间鸿沟的有效方案。

Abstract: Scientific data are increasingly used by AI agents, yet existing dataset representations provide limited support for autonomous discovery, interpretation, and invocation. This limitation stems from the fragmentation of scientific data across heterogeneous repositories and from dataset representations designed primarily for human use. To address this limitation, we introduce the Scientific Data Skill (SciDSK), an agent-ready representation that packages dataset-specific knowledge and operational guidance as a reusable agent skill. A SciDSK integrates dataset descriptions, scientific context, file organization, usage procedures, quality checks, and provenance information while retaining the underlying data in its original repository. We define a structured SciDSK specification and develop a systematic construction pipeline that grounds each SciDSK in authoritative dataset records and associated supporting materials. We further establish the Scientific Data Skill Bank, a unified platform that publishes SciDSK resources across six scientific disciplines and supports package access, persistent identification, and traceability to source datasets. We evaluate SciDSK through a retrieval benchmark for dataset discovery and controlled cases for dataset interpretation. The results show that SciDSK improves agent-driven dataset discovery and provides more precise and actionable support for dataset interpretation. These findings support the value of organizing dataset-specific knowledge in an agent-ready representation.

</details>


### [77] [Can Agent Memory Systems Track Evolving State?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19652)
*Xinyi Fan,Miri Liu,Ruozhen Yang,Siru Ouyang,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StateMemBench基准和StateMem记忆方法，聚焦LLM Agent在长交互中的"状态追踪"能力——即回答必须反映当前世界状态而非被取代的旧状态。StateMem显式追踪状态取代关系和依赖，在DeepSeek-V4-Flash上提升1.8x当前态准确率，并可作为轻量包装器提升多种记忆系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆基准多聚焦于"回忆"型任务，但真实长时、高价值任务中LLM Agent需要追踪不断演化的世界状态（事实、约束、决策被修订后，回答须反映当前状态）。这一能力缺口未被已有基准覆盖，也未被已有记忆系统充分解决。

Method: 1) 定义状态追踪能力并构建StateMemBench基准，含234个多会话场景、两个对话长度区间，采用闭池评分区分当前态/取代态/其他错误；2) 提出StateMem状态优先记忆方法，显式追踪状态取代关系与关系依赖；3) 将状态方法作为轻量单次调用包装器应用于现有记忆/检索系统。
