An earlier qualification cohort likewise yielded 0/10 complete passes: one model met every task-performance gate, but all ten failed ordinary-language preservation, confining the system to explicitly task-gated use.

</details>


### [110] [DECOWAM: Decoupled Whole-Body World-Action Model for Legged Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20114)
*Siyuan Ma,Boshi Zhang,Yutian Zhang,Qinglian Wu,Jiaqi Zhai,Dong Wei,Qiaojun Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DECOWAM，一种全身世界-动作模型，通过分离相机自身运动与基座/手臂动作的专用条件接口，实现移动操作机器人在运动视点下的联合视觉预测与全身控制。配套引入ARMDOG真机数据集，相比FastWAM基线，动作MSE降低21.7%，仅需25.95M可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型主要针对固定基座平台开发，未显式区分相机自身运动与基座和手臂动作对观测变化的影响。在移动操作场景中，机器人需要同时预测运动与手臂动作如何联合改变未来观测并控制自身，因此需要一种能因子分解这些因素的模型。

Method: DECOWAM采用冻结的适配FastWAM骨干网络，训练残差适配器；通过从特权观测中蒸馏的动作等价未来瓶颈(action-equivalent future bottleneck)；对抗式分离基座与手臂隐变量；以及基座速度条件化用于视频预测。ARMDOG数据集同步记录视频、全身状态、动作和语言标注。

Result: 在固定回放协议下，DECOWAM相比FastWAM在未来的视频预测和动作预测上均有提升，动作MSE降低21.7%，仅需25.95M可训练适配参数。在每种方法79次闭环试验中，DECOWAM实现了最高的全身协调性和基座位移鲁棒性，任务完成率与最优基线相当。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明，体现感知的因子分解(embodiment-aware factorization)能够在移动视点下以参数高效的方式支持联合视觉预测与全身控制，为移动操作机器人的世界模型提供了一条有效路径。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation requires a robot to predict how locomotion and arm motion jointly alter future observations and control. Existing world-action models, developed largely for fixed-base platforms, do not explicitly distinguish camera ego-motion from base and arm actions. Here we introduce DECOWAM, a whole-body world-action model that separates these factors through dedicated conditional interfaces. DECOWAM freezes an adapted FastWAM backbone and trains residual adapters, an action-equivalent future bottleneck distilled from privileged observations, adversarially separated base and arm latents, and base-velocity conditioning for video prediction. We further introduce ARMDOG, a real-robot dataset that synchronizes video, whole-body state and action, and language. On a fixed replay protocol, DECOWAM improved both future-video and action prediction over FastWAM, reducing action MSE by 21.7% with 25.95M trainable adaptation parameters. Across 79 closed-loop trials per method, it achieved the highest observed whole-body coordination and base-displacement robustness among the compared systems, while task completion remained comparable to the strongest baseline. These results show that embodiment-aware factorization can support parameter-efficient joint visual prediction and whole-body control under moving viewpoints.

</details>


### [111] [DARS: Dual-Level Credit Assignment RL with Structured Reasoning for Instruction-Based Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20161)
*Haoxiang Cao,Jiajiong Cao,Xuanpu Zhang,Changqian Yu,Chaoqun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DARS是一个用于指令式图像编辑的双模块（规划器-渲染器）强化学习框架，通过跨模块和模块内的双层信用分配实现更高效的训练，在五个基准上超越联合RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规划器-渲染器流水线的强化学习仅使用最终图像奖励进行训练，效率低下——无法区分优化应侧重于规划器还是渲染器，且自由格式推理轨迹中难以定位失败原因。

Method: DARS包含两个层面的信用分配：(1) 跨模块层：通过多计划多渲染展开估计计划间和计划内奖励变异性，实现软模块路由，并利用滚动平均奖励提供难度估计以构建自适应课程；(2) 模块内层：规划器采用四字段结构化推理输出，通过前缀门控奖励和token级优势重加权，将结果级反馈转化为局部监督。

Result: 在五个基准上的实验表明，DARS在使用相同骨干网络、数据、奖励模型和滚动预算的情况下优于联合RL基线，在推理密集型编辑任务上收益最大。

Conclusion: 通过双层次的信用分配（跨模块的软路由与自适应课程，以及模块内的结构化推理与token级监督），DARS有效解决了双阶段图像编辑系统中奖励稀疏和信用分配模糊的问题。

Abstract: Instruction-based image editing uses a planner-renderer pipeline: a vision-language model (VLM) first converts the instruction into an edit plan, and a diffusion model then executes that plan. Training such systems with only final-image rewards is inefficient because a poor edit does not reveal whether additional optimization should place more emphasis on the planner or the renderer, and even planner-dominant cases remain difficult to localize within a free-form reasoning trace. We present DARS, a reinforcement learning framework for dual-level credit assignment in this two-stage setting. Across modules, multi-plan multi-render rollouts estimate between-plan and within-plan reward variability for soft module routing, while rollout mean rewards provide hardness estimates for an adaptive curriculum. Within the planner, a four-field structured reasoning output enables a prefix-gated reward and token-level advantage reweighting, turning outcome-level feedback into localized supervision. Experiments on five benchmarks show that DARS outperforms a Joint~RL baseline with the same backbone, data, reward model, and rollout budget, with the largest gains on reasoning-intensive edits.

</details>


### [112] [The Third Restructuring of Software Form: From the Three-Tier Architecture to Storage, Models, and Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20201)
*Wei Lin,Tao Zhou,Zhaofei Xie,Changgui Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出软件正经历第三次范式转移——Software 3.0，即由上下文和推理决定行为。其终极形态收敛为三大要素：通用数据库（统一持久状态与记忆抽象）、大模型（推理与生成的核心智能）和代理（连接前两者的执行循环）。在传统三层架构中，UI层被模型按需生成界面能力吸收，业务逻辑层按"可表达性×关键性"重新划分给模型推理与存储约束，仅数据层被提升为唯一持久基础设施。论文形式化了这一收敛论点，提出最小参考架构，通过原型和实际模型提供证据，并系统分析其成立条件与失效边界（确定性、成本、安全、可验证性）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件形态经历了从"指令决定行为"（Software 1.0）到"数据决定行为"（Software 2.0/机器学习）的两次范式转移。随着大模型和智能代理的发展，作者观察到第三次转移正在发生——由上下文和推理决定行为（Software 3.0），并试图对这一转移进行系统性形式化论证，预测软件架构的未来形态及其对开发者、数据库行业和软件工程学科的影响。

Method: 作者形式化提出了软件3.0的收敛论点（convergence thesis），定义了由通用数据库、大模型和代理组成的最小参考架构；分析了传统三层架构中各层如何被重新划分与吸收；并通过真实原型和实际运行模型的证据进行验证；同时系统分析论点成立的条件（可表达、可验证、外部有状态、工具完备）以及失效的边界（确定性、成本、安全性、可验证性）。

Result: 论文证明该论点在"可表达、可验证、外部有状态、工具完备"的任务域中成立；传统架构中UI层将消失（被模型按需生成吸收），业务逻辑层按"可表达性×关键性"被重新划分——可由模型推理的部分交给模型，必需严格约束的部分降为存储约束，残余确定性逻辑保留为工具，数据层则成为唯一持久基础设施。

Conclusion: Software 3.0将重塑开发者角色、数据库行业和软件工程学科本身。架构的稳定终端形态是数据库、大模型与代理三要素的组合，但该论点存在明确的应用边界——在需要确定性的场景、成本敏感场景、安全敏感场景以及无法验证结果的场景中，该收敛论点不成立，传统架构仍将保留。

Abstract: Software form has undergone two paradigm shifts since its inception: Software 1.0, in which instructions determine behavior, and Software 2.0, in which data determines behavior (machine learning). This paper argues that a third shift - Software 3.0, in which context and reasoning determine behavior - is now underway, and contends that its terminal form converges to three elements: a generalized database (the unified abstraction of all persistent state and memory), a large model (the intelligence core that performs reasoning and generation), and an agent (the execution loop connecting the first two). The core argument is as follows: in the traditional three-tier architecture, the user-interface layer will be absorbed by the model's ability to generate interfaces on demand, the business-logic layer will be re-partitioned along "expressibility x criticality" into model reasoning and storage constraints (with residual deterministic logic retained as tools), and only the data layer will be elevated into the sole persistent infrastructure. We formalize this convergence thesis, present a minimal reference architecture, report evidence from real prototypes and a live model, and systematically analyze both the conditions under which it holds and the boundaries where it fails - determinism, cost, security, and verifiability delimit the thesis's domain of applicability. We argue that the thesis holds in task domains that are expressible, verifiable, externally stateful, and tool-complete, and that it will reshape the roles of developers, the database industry, and the software-engineering discipline.

</details>


### [113] [MemTrapBench: Benchmarking Cognitive Traps in LLM Memory Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20202)
*Mengru Wang,Haozhe Luo,Zhenqian Xu,Zhixiang Cui,Haoming Xu,Qu Yang,Jizhan Fang,Junfeng Fang,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MemTrapBench基准，用于系统性评估大语言模型中的"记忆诱导认知陷阱"——即使记忆被正确提取和存贮且语义相关，仍可能扭曲模型推理或信念并降低当前任务性能。实验发现所有现有记忆策略均不如无记忆设置，最强方法也有超过10%的性能下降。为此提出AdaptiveMem推理期方法，在缓解认知陷阱的同时保持或提升标准记忆基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆基准主要评估信息是否正确提取、存储和检索，忽略了对检索到的记忆如何重塑模型推理并影响当前任务性能的考查。作者识别出记忆诱导的认知陷阱：即使记忆被忠实记录且语义相关，仍可能扭曲模型的推理或信念，降低当前任务表现，而这一失效模式缺乏系统性评估。

Method: 1) 构建MemTrapBench基准，覆盖两类认知陷阱：推理固着（Reasoning Fixation）和信念扭曲（Belief Distortion）。2) 提出AdaptiveMem，一种简单有效的推理期方法，通过指令引导大语言模型避免记忆陷阱。

Result: 在两类模型家族和五个代表性记忆框架上的实验表明，MemTrapBench具有挑战性：所有被评估的记忆策略均不如无记忆设置，即使最强方法也遭受超过10%的性能下降。AdaptiveMem在MemTrapBench上缓解了认知陷阱，同时在多种记忆框架的标准记忆基准上保持或提升了性能。

Conclusion: 记忆检索虽然重要，但可能对模型当前任务的推理产生负面影响。MemTrapBench揭示了这一普遍存在的认知陷阱问题，AdaptiveMem提供了一种简单有效的缓解方案，为记忆系统的设计提供了新的视角和评估方向。

Abstract: Memory has become a key component of large language models, enabling them to retain information and learn from long-term interactions. However, existing memory benchmarks mainly evaluate whether information is correctly extracted, stored, and retrieved, while largely overlooking how retrieved memories reshape model reasoning and affect performance on the current task. We identify memory-induced cognitive traps: even faithfully recorded and semantically relevant memories can distort model reasoning or beliefs and degrade current task performance. To systematically evaluate these failure modes, we introduce MemTrapBench, which covers two forms of cognitive traps: Reasoning Fixation and Belief Distortion. Experiments across two model families and five representative memory frameworks show that MemTrapBench is challenging: all evaluated memory strategies underperform the no-memory setting, with even the strongest methods suffering drops of more than 10%. To mitigate these cognitive traps, we propose AdaptiveMem, a simple yet effective inference-time method that instructs LLMs to avoid memory traps. AdaptiveMem mitigates cognitive traps on MemTrapBench while preserving or improving performance on standard memory benchmarks across diverse memory frameworks.

</details>


### [114] [ContractScrub: A benchmark for final review of legal contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20204)
*Yejin Bang,Kirsty Fielding,Brandan Oliver,Brian Birke,Nabeel Seedat,Andrew M. Bean*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ContractScrub——首个评估法律合同"清洗"（scrubbing）能力的基准测试，包含由资深律师手工构造的、覆盖多种错误类别的合同。实验发现前沿LLM在该任务上表现意外地差，最强模型仅达到0.75宏平均召回率，尽管其在通用基准上表现优异，揭示了当前模型的实际局限及领域特定基准的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合同清洗是法律行业中最适合自动化的任务之一（常规、繁琐、需长文档细节关注），且在理论上与前沿LLM的长上下文推理、一致性检查和命名实体识别能力高度契合。然而，尽管该任务具有经济价值和自动化潜力，此前从未有人对LLM在此任务上的表现进行正式评估，缺乏相应的基准测试。

Method: 构建ContractScrub基准：由经验丰富的律师手工撰写合同，覆盖多样化错误类别（如定义术语误用、错误引用、语言不一致等），用以系统评估LLM的合同清洗能力。

Result: 前沿模型在ContractScrub上表现意外地差，只有1个模型达到了0.75的宏平均召回率，尽管这些模型在看似相关的通用基准上表现强劲。

Conclusion: 当前前沿LLM在实际合同清洗任务上的能力远低于预期，说明通用基准上的表现并不能可靠预测领域特定任务的真实效果，强调需要针对狭隘、领域特定的基准来衡量实际现实世界影响。

Abstract: Legal work, with its heavy reliance on processing large amounts of text, is often considered one of the domains most exposed to the use of LLMs. Contract ``scrubbing,'' the final review of transactional agreements for errors and inconsistencies, is a particularly suitable task for automation, because it is routine, painstaking work requiring detailed attention to long documents. Scrubbing also seems to align naturally with the general capabilities expected of frontier LLMs around long-context reasoning, consistency checking, and named entity recognition (NER). Despite the economic value and potential for automation, no formal evaluations of LLMs performing contract scrubbing have been conducted. We introduce ContractScrub, the first benchmark designed to evaluate contract scrubbing capabilities, comprising contracts hand-crafted by experienced lawyers over diverse error categories such as misuse of defined terms, incorrect references, and inconsistent language. Frontier models perform surprisingly poorly with only one model reaching 0.75 macro average recall despite strong performance on seemingly related general benchmarks, demonstrating the practical limits of current models and the importance of narrowly targeted, domain-specific benchmarks for measuring real-world impact.

</details>


### [115] [Electronic Navigational Chart Change Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20218)
*Jacob Arndt,Abhishek Potnis,Alexandre Sorokine*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化分类电子海图（ENC）变更的方法，通过将复杂的矢量数据变更编码为结构化表格格式，并结合空间上下文编码器和属性编码器，利用梯度提升树在两个运营数据集上分别达到90%和94%的准确率，验证了机器学习在海上安全维护中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水文局面临的关键挑战是判断图表变更对海上安全构成关键还是非关键风险。现有工作流严重依赖人工审查和验证，劳动强度大、难以应对大规模更新、且分析人员之间存在不一致性，亟需自动化解决方案。

Method: 提出一个自动化分类ENC变更的方法：1）建立基线编码方案，将复杂矢量数据变更转换为结构化表格格式；2）核心编码方案包括空间上下文编码器（丰富变更表示与周围地理特征）和ENC属性编码器（表示修改对象的属性-值描述）；3）使用调优的梯度提升树模型进行分类。

Result: 在两个运营数据集上（共1,308对图表、超过100,000个单独图表修改）进行评估，调优后的梯度提升树分别达到90%和94%的准确率，相比默认超参数模型（未使用空间上下文和属性嵌入）提升了5-7%。简单的位置和空间聚合方法已被证明有效，为更复杂的空间表示学习方法提供了评估基础。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明将机器学习集成到运营地理空间管线是可行的，能够改善ENC维护并增强海上安全。同时，简单位置和空间聚合方法的有效性为未来探索更先进的空间表示学习技术奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Electronic Navigational Charts (ENCs) are geospatial vector datasets used in maritime navigation systems that represent hydrographic and navigational information such as depths, navigational aids, traffic schemes, and hazards. A major challenge for hydrographic offices is determining whether a given chart change poses a critical or non-critical risk to maritime safety. Existing workflows rely heavily on manual review and verification, which is labor-intensive, scales poorly with the volume of incoming chart updates, and introduces inter-analyst inconsistencies. To address this challenge, we propose a method for automated classification of ENC changes. We establish a baseline encoding scheme to translate complex vector data changes into a structured tabular format for classification models. The two crucial components of the encoding scheme include a spatial context encoder to enrich the change representations with surrounding geographic features, and an ENC attribute encoder to represent nuanced attribute-value descriptions of the modified objects. We evaluate the proposed approach across two distinct operational datasets, comprising 1,308 chart pairs containing over 100,000 individual chart modifications. Tuned gradient-boosted trees leveraging the proposed encoding schemes achieve accuracies of 90% and 94% on the two datasets, yielding a 5-7% improvement over default hyperparameterized models trained on encodings without spatial context and attribute embeddings. These results demonstrate the viability of integrating machine learning into operational geospatial pipelines to improve ENC maintenance and enhance maritime safety. Finally, our experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of simple location and spatial aggregation methods, providing a foundation for evaluating more sophisticated spatial representation learning techniques for this application.

</details>


### [116] [InsufficiencyBench: Evaluating LLM legal advice on underspecified user queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20220)
*Samuel J. Vincent,Daniel Calloway,Fangyi Yu,Andrew M. Bean,Nabeel Seedat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出InsufficiencyBench，首个针对法律查询"信息不足"问题的基准，考察模型是否能识别查询中缺失的关键法律要素、指出缺失内容并及时止损而非过早下结论。基于8类缺失要素分类法与3类结构失效模式，构建202项基准数据，评测10个前沿模型后发现表现普遍不佳（F2≤0.46，中位召回率0.44）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律AI基准假设查询输入是完全指定的，但实际场景中用户常遗漏对法律结果具有决定性影响的事实信息。缺乏专门基准来评估模型是否能够识别查询侧的信息不足问题，导致模型可能在缺乏关键要素的情况下给出误导性结论。

Method: 提出一个包含八类缺失要素类别的分类法，涵盖三种结构性失效模式（switch切换、gating门控、fatal prerequisite致命前提）；构建202个基准条目（58个基础查询和144个缺陷变体），覆盖6个法律领域和24个美国司法辖区，由执业律师标注；评估10个前沿模型。

Result: 没有任何模型在缺失要素识别上超过F2=0.46，中位召回率为0.44。模型要么不加区分地模糊回应，要么在虚构预设条件下静默作答。没有模型能在处理缺陷查询时同时做到识别与限定回答，同时又能直接回答完整查询。

Conclusion: 现有法律AI系统无法可靠地识别查询中缺失的关键法律信息，存在系统性缺陷。这提示需要专门的训练和评估来提升模型在"识别缺失信息"和"克制过早结论"方面的能力。

Abstract: Legal AI systems are increasingly used to answer legal questions, yet existing benchmarks assume queries arrive fully specified. In practice, users omit facts that materially determine the legal outcome. We introduce InsufficiencyBench, the first legal benchmark targeting query-side insufficiency: whether a model recognizes when a query lacks legally material information, identifies what is missing, and refrains from premature conclusions. We formalize a taxonomy of eight canonical missing-element categories across three structural failure modes---switch, gating, and fatal prerequisite--- and construct 202 benchmark items (58 base queries, 144 deficient variants) spanning six legal domains and 24 US jurisdictions and annotated by practising attorneys. Evaluating ten frontier models, we find that no model exceeds F2 = 0.46 on missing-element identification and that the median recall is 0.44. Models either hedge indiscriminately or answer silently under fabricated presumptions. No model both identifies and qualifies responses to deficient queries while directly addressing complete ones.

</details>


### [117] [Rule-Compliant Visual Spatial Planning for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20237)
*Yu Chen,Ting Lei,Yaoyi Li,Jia Cai,Zhecen Wu,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RuleMaze基准测试和DMP方法，用于评估和提升多模态大语言模型在显式及未见规则约束下的视觉空间规划能力，实验证明DMP较端到端基线在规则遵循和规划成功率上有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型虽具备语言推理与视觉感知能力，但其在显式规则约束（尤其是未见过的规则）下的视觉空间规划能力尚未被充分探索。现有研究缺乏可控的基准来系统性地评估模型在空间布局理解、自然语言规则解读和约束下动作规划三方面的联合能力。

Method: 提出RuleMaze可控基准测试，要求MLLM在遵循不同复杂度自然语言规则的前提下导航迷宫。具体包含两大技术创新：(1) Language-Logic-Function Hybridization，自动生成自然语言规则并翻译为逻辑表示和可执行验证器，实现规则的可扩展系统性构建；(2) Disentangled Multimodal Planning (DMP)，将感知、执行和规则验证分离为可解释的推理原语，通过解耦实现向更复杂和未见规则的系统泛化，并提供透明的中间规划轨迹。

Result: 实验表明DMP在规则遵循和规划成功率上显著优于端到端文本规划基线，展示了在复杂及未见规则下的系统泛化能力。

Conclusion: RuleMaze为研究MLLM中基于规则的可解释空间规划建立了原则性基准，DMP通过推理组件解耦有效提升了规则遵循和规划性能，为多模态智能体的基础规划能力研究提供了重要平台。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) combine linguistic reasoning with visual perception, yet their ability to perform visual spatial planning under explicit or previously unseen rule constraints remains underexplored. This setting requires models to jointly understand spatial layouts, interpret natural-language rules, and plan valid actions accordingly. To address this gap, we introduce RuleMaze, a controllable benchmark in which MLLMs must navigate mazes while obeying natural-language rules of varying complexity. RuleMaze isolates rule-compliant spatial planning by requiring accurate perception, rule interpretation, and constrained action planning. To enable scalable and systematic rule construction, we propose Language-Logic-Function Hybridization, which automatically generates natural-language rules and translates them into logical representations and executable validators, eliminating manual rule engineering. To improve rule following and generalization, we introduce Disentangled Multimodal Planning (DMP), which separates perception, execution, and rule verification through interpretable reasoning primitives. By disentangling these components, DMP facilitates systematic generalization to more complex and previously unseen rules, while providing transparent intermediate planning traces. Experiments demonstrate that DMP substantially improves rule compliance and planning success compared to end-to-end textual planning baselines. Overall, RuleMaze establishes a principled benchmark for studying grounded and interpretable rule-based spatial planning in MLLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/oceanflowlab/RuleMaze.

</details>


### [118] [QUASAR: A Quantum-Classical Neural Network for SAR Satellite Physical-Layer Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20240)
*Vincenzo Sammartino,Nathanael Denis,Roberto Di Pietro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QUASAR是首个将CNN频谱图编码器与变分量子电路(VQC)融合的量子-经典混合架构，用于X波段SAR卫星信号的物理层认证(PLA)。它比经典机器学习更数据高效（仅需10%训练数据即可达到同等精度），并在等数据预算下提升分类精度。在重放攻击、IQ注入和星载欺骗三种对抗场景下，分别以89.7%、94.1%和81.3%的成功率拒绝欺骗传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X波段SAR卫星(8-12 GHz)在灾害响应、环境监测和军事情报中至关重要，但缺乏鲁棒的物理层认证。现有PLA系统多基于射频指纹，仅适用于6 GHz以下频段，且依赖经典深度学习，难以充分拟合区分卫星硬件的IQ相位非线性特征。

Method: 提出QUASAR架构，将CNN频谱图编码器与变分量子电路(VQC)融合，构成量子-经典的混合分类架构，用于X波段SAR信号的物理层认证。在三种对抗场景（重放攻击、crafted-IQ注入、星载欺骗）下进行测试。

Result: QUASAR在重放、IQ注入和星载欺骗三种场景下分别拒绝了89.7%、94.1%和81.3%的欺骗传输。仅需经典基线10%的训练数据即可达到同等精度，且在等数据预算下分类精度优于经典基线。

Conclusion: QUASAR建立了首个面向卫星星座的量子增强物理层分类器，展示了量子-经典混合架构在物理层认证中的新研究方向，且该框架和结果本身具有独立研究价值。

Abstract: X-band SAR satellites (8-12 GHz) play a critical role in disaster response, environmental monitoring, and military intelligence. Yet, they lack robust physical-layer authentication (PLA), a security layer orthogonal to cryptographic solutions. Existing PLA systems, typically based on radio-frequency fingerprinting, are often limited to sub-6 GHz frequencies and rely on classical deep learning. However, this approach underfits the IQ phase nonlinearities that distinguish satellite hardware. In this paper, we present QUASAR, to the best of our knowledge the first quantum-classical hybrid architecture that fuses a CNN spectrogram encoder with a variational quantum circuit (VQC) to provide PLA to X-band SAR signals. Our solution enjoys two distinctive features: (i) it is markedly more data-efficient than classical machine learning, requiring only 10% of the training data to match the accuracy of classical baselines -- data collection being notoriously the most time-consuming phase of PLA; and, (ii) at an equal data budget, it improves classification accuracy over those baselines. In detail, we test our solution under three adversarial scenarios: replay, crafted-IQ injection, and space-borne spoofing. QUASAR rejects spoofed transmissions in 89.7%, 94.1%, and 81.3% of attempts, respectively, establishing the first quantum-enhanced physical-layer classifier for satellite constellations. The fully detailed framework and the supporting results, other than being interesting on their own, show a novel research avenue for physical-layer authentication.

</details>


### [119] [Learning When to Think: Adaptive Reasoning for Test-Time Compute Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20256)
*Gijs Kassenaar,Zhao Yang,Vincent François-Lavet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种让推理语言模型自适应分配推理预算的方法：模型通过输出响应首token在NoThink/Short/Long三种模式中选择，使用GRPO联合学习模式选择和推理，无需独立路由器。在1.5B模型上，该方法在保持准确率基本不变的同时将平均响应长度降低41%，并能迁移到其他基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理语言模型在强化学习训练下通常使用固定的token预算，导致简单问题上过度计算、困难问题上计算不足。作者希望让模型自己学会按问题难度动态分配推理努力，而非人为设定固定推理长度。

Method: 模型响应首token即为模式选择（NoThink/Short/Long），该选择在GRPO内部与推理过程联合学习。通过塑造奖励使每种模式在特定响应长度下各有价值，并设置各模式硬性token上限以保持模式区分度。训练使用1.5B蒸馏模型在MATH数据集上进行。

Result: 三种模式成功涌现且未坍缩为单一选择；较短模式比Long更准确，说明路由器按难度而非随机排序问题。平均三粒种子，MATH500上准确率保持（0.782 vs 基线0.796），平均响应长度从4796降至2811 token（减少41%）。在GSM8K上实现76%的token缩减且准确率不低于类似长度的基线，无需重训即可迁移到其他基准。

Conclusion: 作者构建了一个能按问题自适应决定推理程度的推理模型，在几乎不损失准确率的前提下显著减少了计算开销，并且该能力可泛化到未见过的基准任务上。

Abstract: Reasoning language models trained with reinforcement learning typically operate under a fixed token budget rather than an explicitly adaptive one, which can lead to over-computation on easy problems and insufficient computation on difficult ones. We study whether a model can learn to allocate its own reasoning effort by choosing, as the first token of its response, one of three modes: \textsc{NoThink} (answer as quickly as possible), \textsc{Short} (brief reasoning), or \textsc{Long} (extended reasoning). The choice is learned inside Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with no separate router, through a shaped reward that makes each mode worthwhile at a different response length, together with hard per-mode token caps that keep the modes distinct. On a 1.5B distilled model trained on MATH, the three modes emerge without collapsing to a single choice, and the brief modes end up more accurate than \textsc{Long}, which shows that the router sorts problems by difficulty rather than at random. Averaged over three seeds, the resulting policy stays close to the base model's accuracy on the held-out MATH500 ($0.782$ vs.\ $0.796$) while cutting the mean response length from $4{,}796$ to $2{,}811$ tokens (a $41\%$ reduction). Interestingly, it also transfers to other benchmarks without retraining, with the largest savings where problems are easier, with for instance 76\% token reduction on GSM8K and at higher accuracy than the baselines at similar response length. In short, we build a reasoning model that adaptively chooses how much to reason for each problem.

</details>


### [120] [Catching the Rug: Early Prediction of Fraudulent Memecoins on Solana via Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20271)
*Jianghai Li,Pavel Kuznetsov,Yury Yanovich,Konstantin Nott-Whaley,Igor Vodolazov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究首次在Solana生态中开展大规模（640万代币）的rug pull早期检测，发现多数memecoin在发布一小时内即呈现rug pull特征，并证明仅使用前5分钟交易数据的XGBoost模型即可实现稳健检测，且跨平台数据融合（PumpFun与Raydium）能有效缓解域迁移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solana是memecoin交易量和代币数量领先的区块链，但其rug pull特征与Ethereum不同，主要以流动性操纵和社会动态驱动，缺乏代码级特征可供利用。已有研究集中在Ethereum，Solana生态的rug pull早期检测研究尚属空白，亟需建立有效的检测框架。

Method: 构建包含640万代币、覆盖7个月数据的大型数据集；结合市场分析揭示rug pull的时间分布特征；使用经典机器学习模型（重点为XGBoost）基于前5分钟交易数据进行检测；通过跨平台（PumpFun与Raydium）的数据融合实验评估域迁移问题的缓解效果。

Result: 绝大多数memecoin在发布一小时内即表现出rug pull特征；在缺少代码级特征的情况下，XGBoost仅利用前5分钟交易数据即可获得稳健的检测性能；跨平台多源数据融合显著降低了域迁移影响，提升了检测可靠性。

Conclusion: 该研究推进了对高吞吐量链上DeFi欺诈的理解，为Solana生态的投资者保护提供了实用的早期检测框架，并验证了跨平台数据融合策略在提升检测泛化能力方面的有效性。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of memecoins on blockchain platforms has increased the risk of fraudulent activities, particularly rug pulls. While previous studies have focused on Ethereum-based tokens, this paper shifts the spotlight to Solana, the leading blockchain for memecoins by trading volume and token count. Unlike Ethereum, where rug pulls often exploit smart contract backdoors, Solana memecoin rug pulls are predominantly driven by liquidity manipulation and social dynamics. This research pioneers large-scale rug pull early detection in the Solana ecosystem by assembling a dataset of 6.4 million tokens over 7 months. Market analysis reveals that a vast majority of these memecoins exhibit rug pull characteristics within one hour of launch, highlighting the urgency of short-horizon prediction. Despite the absence of code-level features, we demonstrate that classic machine learning models, particularly Gradient Boosting (XGBoost), achieve robust performance in detecting potential rug pulls using only the first 5 minutes of trading data. Furthermore, we evaluate cross-platform generalization between PumpFun and Raydium, revealing that multi-source data fusion significantly mitigates domain shift and improves detection reliability. This study advances the understanding of DeFi fraud on high-throughput chains and provides a practical framework for protecting investors.

</details>


### [121] [Break It Down, Pass It On: Cross-Task Skill Transfer in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20274)
*Yiyang Feng,Biddut Sarker Bijoy,Niranjan Balasubramanian,Jiawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对照实验系统研究了大语言模型智能体诱导技能的转移可靠性，发现子任务级别的技能诱导优于任务级别，文本格式的技能优于代码格式，并提出一个只需技能和任务描述即可计算的技能效用评分，作为技能迁移前的实用诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体可以从已完成任务中诱导技能并复用，但技能迁移可能不可靠甚至对检索它的智能体造成伤害。何时技能能可靠迁移仍然是一个开放问题，需要系统性的理解和研究。

Method: 进行综合对照研究，沿两个维度比较技能诱导方式：(1)任务级别vs子任务级别的技能诱导；(2)文本vs代码技能格式。在此基础上定义两个互补属性——特异性（衡量技能与真实任务的匹配程度）和抽象性（衡量技能相关性在任务间的均匀分布程度），并进一步提出将两者结合为技能效用评分。

Result: 任务级技能大多使智能体性能低于无记忆基线，而子任务级技能平均提升性能；文本技能比代码技能迁移效果更好。特异性或抽象性单独无法预测任务成功，但两者的联合效应（技能效用评分）与任务成功率一致相关；子任务级和文本技能得分更高。

Conclusion: 技能效用评分计算仅需技能和任务描述，无需任务执行，因此可在任何新任务运行之前作为技能记忆的实用诊断工具，帮助预判技能迁移的有效性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can induce skills from completed tasks and reuse them later to grow more capable with experience. In practice, induced skills may transfer unreliably and can even harm the agent that retrieves them. When agent-induced skills transfer reliably across tasks remains an open question. We conduct a comprehensive and controlled study of how the way skills are induced shapes their transfer across tasks. Specifically, we compare task-level with subtask-level skill induction and text with code skill formats, the two axes along which existing methods differ. Task-level skills mostly reduce the agent's performance below its no-memory baseline while subtask-level skills raise it above on average, and text skills transfer better than code skills. To further understand our findings, we examine two complementary properties of the induced skills: specificity, which measures how closely a skill matches real tasks, and abstractness, which measures how evenly its relevance spreads across tasks. Neither property alone predicts task success, but their combined effect does, which we propose as a skill utility score. The score correlates consistently with task success when skills are transferred, and subtask-level and text skills score higher. Computing skill utility only needs the skills and task descriptions but not any task execution, so our score serves as a practical diagnostic of a skill memory before any new task runs.

</details>


### [122] [Phantom Gains: Auditing Self-Improvement Against a Measured Null](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20290)
*Cheng Xu,Nan Yan,Liming Chen,M-Tahar Kechadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出在语言模型自我改进评估中，均值准确率不足以衡量改进，而应追踪个体问题的得失转变。作者通过审计Qwen3-8B的rank-32 LoRA自我训练的3轮实验（对照冻结控制组），识别出7种测量失败，每种失败在缺少对照时都会反转结论。论文用每问题精确检验替代天然阈值修复，在FDR控制下于所有重复实验中无检出，并提出过渡级审计需要为每个统计量单独测量的零假设基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估LM是否自我改进正从平均准确率转向个体问题层面的得失追踪，但追踪这种转变需要差分两个有噪声的估计值，容易受测量伪影影响。现有方法（如单次贪心解码构建的账本、扩张统计量）在没有对照的情况下会产生虚假的能力变化，因此需要严格审计测量协议本身。

Method: 在Qwen3-8B上进行三轮rank-32 LoRA自我训练，并让冻结控制组通过相同流水线。审计过程中识别7种测量失败，每种在缺少对照时反转结论。提出用基于FDR控制的每问题精确检验替代自然阈值修复，对照用多臂研究已有的基线重复构建零假设。同时构建不同训练臂（外部蒸馏vs三种自我训练）的阶梯比较，并用回归检验不对称性。

Result: 单次贪心解码生成的账本在未训练模型上制造出能力变化（主要源于推理批处理伪影）；扩张统计量给同一模型分配了0.280的获取率。自然阈值修复在重复实验上不成立（其零假设非零）。提出的精确检验在任意保留重复上无检出，且对多重检验规则、错误率和池大小不变。外部蒸馏改善了基模型罕见触及的问题，而三种自我训练均未做到；回归拒绝该不对称性是蒸馏总体增益更大造成的（p<10^-8）。在基模型从未触及的小集合上证据不确定，而自我训练在基线已解决问题上的破坏率远超测量下限。

Conclusion: 过渡级审计需要为每个报告的统计量单独测量零假设，这些零假设可由多臂研究已有的基线重复构建而不增加新实验成本，但不能只用太少的重复。

Abstract: Whether a language model has improved itself is increasingly judged not by mean accuracy but by which individual problems it gains and loses. Tracking these transitions means differencing two noisy estimates, leaving them vulnerable to measurement artifacts. Auditing three rounds of rank-$32$ LoRA self-training on Qwen3-8B against a frozen control pushed through the identical pipeline, we identify seven measurement failures, each of which inverts a reported finding when its control is absent. Several are standard practice. A ledger built on a single greedy decode manufactures capability changes on an untrained model, largely an artifact of inference batching; the expansion statistic separating acquisition from sharpening assigns that same model a rate of $0.280$. The natural threshold repair does not survive replication: estimated across the frozen comparisons such a design already contains, its null stays non-zero. We replace it with a per-problem exact test against a pooled baseline under false-discovery-rate control, which detects nothing on any held-out replicate and is unchanged under the multiple-testing rule, error rate and pool size. Applied to a ladder of arms matched in stream, volume and evaluation, the audit finds that external distillation improves problems the base model rarely reaches while three forms of self-training do not; a regression rejects this asymmetry as a by-product of distillation's larger overall gain ($p < 10^{-8}$). On the far smaller set of problems the base model never reaches, the evidence is inconclusive, while self-training corrupts problems solved at baseline at rates well above the measured floor. Transition-level auditing therefore requires a separately measured null for every statistic it reports: nulls that cost no new experiments, built from baseline replicates a multi-arm study already owns, though not from as few as most possess.

</details>


### [123] [MidTool: Mid-training Data Synthesis for Agentic Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20314)
*Fengqing Jiang,Yite Wang,Boyi Liu,Zhaoyang Wang,Canwen Xu,Zhewei Yao,Radha Poovendran,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MidTool，一个面向智能体工具使用能力的中间训练语料构建流水线，通过整合大规模网页、PDF、代码数据以及来自真实工具API、MCP技能和文档驱动工作流的合成监督数据，对Qwen3模型进行中间训练，显著提升其在BFCL、tau2-Bench和MCP Universe等下游基准上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中间训练已被证明对提升大语言模型的推理密集型能力（如数学、科学）和软件工程中的智能体能力至关重要，但针对通用工具使用这一并行且较少探索的智能体能力的研究仍然缺乏。本文希望填补这一空白，验证通用工具使用是否也能从专门的中间训练中获益。

Method: 提出MidTool流水线构建MidTool-Mix语料，涵盖三类数据源：(1)大规模网页、PDF和代码数据；(2)来自真实工具API和MCP技能的合成监督数据；(3)文档驱动工作流数据。设计目标包括教会模型识别工具可及性（affordance）、从上下文中进行参数接地、组合工具调用工作流以及从不完整信息中恢复。在Qwen3-4B-Base和Qwen3-8B-Base上进行中间训练，随后采用有监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）两阶段后训练。

Result: 与基线相比，MidTool-Mix在SFT和RL两种后训练设置下均持续提升了模型在BFCL、tau2-Bench和MCP Universe三个基准上的下游表现。

Conclusion: 通用工具使用与其他重要的大语言模型能力一样，应当获得专门的中间训练，而不是完全留给后训练阶段处理。MidTool证明了针对工具使用的中间训练可以显著增强模型在这一关键智能体能力上的表现。

Abstract: Mid-training is increasingly recognized as a critical stage for shaping the capabilities of large language models. Recent work has shown that targeted mid-training can strengthen reasoning-intensive abilities such as math and science, and can also improve agentic capabilities in software-engineering settings. In this work, we study the parallel but less explored agentic capability: general tool use. We present MidTool, an open corpus construction pipeline for agentic tool-use mid-training that combines large-scale web, PDF, and code data with synthesized supervision from real-world tool APIs, MCP skills, and document-grounded workflows. MidTool is designed to teach models how to recognize tool affordances, ground arguments from context, compose tool call workflow, and recover from incomplete information. We mid-train Qwen3-4B-Base and Qwen3-8B-Base on MidTool-Mix, and then apply follow-up post-training with both supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. Compared with baselines, MidTool-Mix consistently improves downstream performance under both SFT and RL on BFCL, tau2-Bench, and MCP Universe. These results suggest that general tool use, like other important LLM capabilities, benefits from dedicated mid-training rather than being left entirely to post-training.

</details>


### [124] [Pandora's AI Model Routing Box: Efficient Allocation with Costly Value Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20316)
*Adam Fisch,Shubhendu Trivedi,Fantine Huot,William W. Cohen,Michael Kaisers,Mirella Lapata,Kate Larson,Jacob Eisenstein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Pandora's Router和Pandora's Bidder，将异构AI系统中专家模型的价值评估成本与路由质量之间的权衡形式化为Pandora's Box问题，通过信息价值理论决定何时值得投资昂贵的估计器，在匹配穷举估计路由质量的同时大幅减少昂贵估计器的调用次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构AI系统中路由查询需要估计每个专家模型的预期回报，但估计存在成本权衡：廉价估计器（如基于嵌入的预测器）快速但有噪声，昂贵估计器（如可访问检索结果或部分推理轨迹的微调模型）准确但计算开销大。现有方法缺乏对这一权衡的系统性形式化处理。

Method: 将价值估计成本与收益的权衡形式化为Pandora's Box（带代价检查的最优搜索）问题。在高斯信号模型假设下，推导出封闭形式的信息价值表达式，据此构建集中式策略Pandora's Router和去中心化策略Pandora's Bidder。在去中心化场景中，专家独立决定是否投资自我评估后再接受报价认领查询。

Result: 在三个领域（多LLM基准、检索增强专家、可变推理时计算量的LLM）的实验表明，Pandora's Router在路由质量上与穷举估计相匹配，同时大幅减少昂贵估计器的查询次数。在去中心化设置中，当竞争估计准确时，信息价值推理改善分配效率；但当竞争估计有噪声时，可能增加策略性专家的效用而损害他人利益。

Conclusion: Pandora's Box框架为异构AI路由中的价值估计成本与路由质量的权衡提供了理论上最优的形式化方案，集中式策略在实践中高效且有效。去中心化扩展揭示了估计噪声对策略性行为的影响，为多智能体AI生态中的分配机制设计提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Heterogeneous AI systems composed of multiple models, architectures, harnesses, or inference-time settings can improve quality and efficiency by routing queries to the specialist who can answer most effectively at the lowest cost. Routing requires estimating each specialist's expected return, but this value estimation has a cost. Cheap estimators (e.g., embedding-based predictors) are fast but noisy, while accurate estimators (e.g., fine-tuned models with access to retrieval results or partial reasoning traces) are expensive. We formalize this tradeoff as an instance of Pandora's Box, the classical problem of optimal search with costly inspection. Under a Gaussian signal model, the resulting policies have closed-form value-of-information expressions that determine, for each specialist and input, whether refining the value estimate is worth its cost. We call the centralized policy Pandora's Router. We extend this to a decentralized setting, Pandora's Bidder, where specialists independently decide whether to invest in self-assessment before accepting an offered price to claim a query. Experiments across three domains---a standard multi-LLM benchmark, retrieval-augmented specialists, and LLMs with variable inference-time reasoning---show that Pandora's Router matches the routing quality of exhaustive estimation, while querying the expensive estimator far less often. In the decentralized setting, value-of-information reasoning improves allocative efficiency when competing estimates are accurate; when competing estimates are noisy, however, it can increase the strategic specialist's utility at the expense of others.

</details>


### [125] [AI4AI-Bench: Benchmarking LLM Agents in Algorithmic Design for Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20318)
*Yizhe Chi,Wenyi Li,Deyao Hong,Xiaoqiu Wang,Mingju Gao,Kaisen Yang,Bingxiang He,Youjie Zheng,Calvin Xiao,Qinhuai Na*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI4AI-Bench——首个专门评测AI系统设计训练算法能力的基准，包含10个冻结的研究仓库和10类训练算法家族。在29种配置、6个系统上的平均得分仅为0.166，最强系统也只达到0.250，说明当前AI系统即使具备递归自我改进（RSI）的潜力，也远未达到能设计出改进训练算法的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进（RSI）的核心问题是AI系统能否改进产生它的训练算法本身。但现有基准无法隔离评估这一能力——它们要么靠收集数据获胜，要么靠调超参获胜，无法区分"改变运行方式"与"改变模型学习方式"。

Method: 构建AI4AI-Bench：包含10个冻结的研究仓库，覆盖10类训练算法家族。每个任务中，代理有4小时在一张B300 GPU上重写训练算法，然后代码从零重新运行至多12小时，由固定且对代理隐藏的评估器评分，并与仓库原始算法在同一流程下对比。由于10个指标不可通约，所有任务映射到统一尺度：0=无信息模型，0.1=仓库自带算法，1.0=任务最优。

Result: 29种配置、6个系统在所有10个任务上的平均得分为0.166，最强系统仅达0.250。多数提交从未改变模型的学习方式；少数真正修改学习算法的提交平均得分0.226，其余仅0.126。增加推理努力能将修改学习算法的提交比例从8%提升到64%，平均得分从0.094提升到0.196。

Conclusion: 当前最强系统也只完成了仓库已有算法与最优算法之间距离的不到五分之一，表明AI系统设计训练算法的能力严重不足。大部分提交甚至不改变模型学习方式，而额外的推理努力主要带来的是"愿意去改"的意愿提升。作者公开了全部任务、评估器和评分提交，以便在系统演进过程中重复测量。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) asks whether an AI system can improve the process that produces AI systems, so that the next system inherits the improvement. That process is the training algorithm: a better objective or update rule improves the compute\mbox{-}capability exchange rate for every subsequent run, including the one that produces the next agent. Whether RSI is feasible therefore turns on whether an agent can design training algorithms. No benchmark isolates that ability: existing suites are won by collecting data or by tuning hyperparameters, and none tells a change to how a run is executed apart from a change to how the model learns. We present AI4AI\mbox{-}Bench, 10 frozen research repositories spanning 10 training algorithm families. In each task, an agent has 4 hours on one B300 to rewrite the training algorithm; its code is then rerun from scratch for up to 12 hours and scored by a fixed evaluator hidden from the agent, against the repository's original algorithm under the same procedure. Because the 10 metrics are incommensurable, every task is mapped onto one scale on which $0$ is an uninformative model, $0.1$ is the algorithm the repository ships, and $1.0$ is the task optimum. Across 29 configurations of 6 systems on all 10 tasks the mean score is $0.166$, and the best system reaches $0.250$: even the strongest closes under a fifth of the distance between the algorithm that was already there and the optimum. The submissions show where that distance went: most never change how the model learns at all, and the minority that do average $0.226$ against $0.126$ for the rest. More reasoning effort mostly buys the willingness to go there, taking that minority from $8\%$ of submissions to $64\%$ and the mean score from $0.094$ to $0.196$. We release the task suite, the evaluators and every scored submission, so that the measurement can be repeated as these systems change.

</details>


### [126] [An Agentic Approach for Active Data Collection, Travel Behavior Modeling, and Weather-Sensitive Demand Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20320)
*Narges Ahmadi,Yubo Jiao,Jônatas Augusto Manzolli,Jiangbo Yu,Luis Miranda-Moreno*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出一个三智能体工作流，整合对话式数据采集、结构化数据处理与行为预测，通过聊天机器人管理的图像增强陈述偏好调查收集了454条学生通勤者出行模式选择数据，评估了从2到35B参数的九种本地部署大语言模型，发现最佳文本零样本LLM达到69.9%的五分类准确率，视觉增强配置达到71.5%，展示了多智能体工作流在行为研究中的协调潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 出行行为研究中数字数据采集与预测建模常常分离开发与评估，缺乏端到端的统一工作流，本研究旨在弥合这一鸿沟，整合对话式调查、数据处理和预测建模为一个可审计的多智能体框架。

Method: 采用三智能体工作流：(1) 聊天机器人管理的图像增强陈述偏好调查收集出行模式选择数据；(2) 用多元logit模型分析天气相关关联，以逻辑回归和随机森林作为机器学习基准；(3) 在四种零样本提示条件下评估九种本地部署LLM（2-35B参数），并扩展人格设定、少样本和视觉配置。

Result: 随机森林达到69.6%五分类准确率；最佳文本零样本LLM达到69.9%；视觉增强最佳配置达到71.5%准确率。习惯性出行信息带来最一致的增益，专家框架优于角色扮演，少样本提示在少量示例后稳定提升预测性能。

Conclusion: 研究表明对话式调查、结构化数据处理、传统行为建模、机器学习和多模态LLM预测可以在一个可审计的多智能体工作流中协调运行，且视觉上下文可为特定模型提供额外预测信息。

Abstract: Travel behavior research increasingly combines digital data collection with predictive modeling, yet these stages are often developed and evaluated separately. This study proposes a three-agent workflow integrating conversational data collection, structured data processing, and behavioral prediction. A chatbot-administered, image-augmented stated-preference survey collected mode choices from student commuters across five predefined weather scenarios, yielding 454 respondent-scenario observations. Weather-related associations were analyzed using a multinomial logit model, while logistic regression and random forest provided machine-learning benchmarks. Nine locally deployed large language models (LLMs), ranging from 2 to 35 billion parameters, were evaluated across four zero-shot prompt-and-context conditions and extended through persona, few-shot, and vision-based configurations. Random forest achieved 69.6% five-class accuracy, while the best text-only zero-shot LLM reached 69.9% without task-specific fitting. Habitual travel information produced the most consistent gains, Expert framing generally outperformed Role-Play, and persona information was most useful when habitual travel information was unavailable. Few-shot prompting improved prediction for several models, with gains stabilizing after a small number of examples. Using the same weather images shown to respondents, the best vision-based configuration reached 71.5% five-class accuracy, indicating that visual context may provide additional predictive information for selected models. Overall, the study shows how conversational surveys, structured data processing, conventional behavioral modeling, machine learning, and multimodal LLM prediction can be coordinated within an auditable multi-agent workflow.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Towards On-Board Implementation of ML-Based Helicopter Weight Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19210)
*Nicolas Valot,Ammar Mechouche,Benjamin Lesage,Claire Pagetti,Louis Fabre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于监督机器学习的直升机起飞重量估算模型，利用空客全球在役机队的大规模数据，遵循EASA机器学习应用概念文件及Eurocae ED-324标准，采用LSTM循环神经网络实现，并在传统航空电子计算机上验证了其部署可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直升机起飞重量是影响飞行安全和性能的关键参数，需要精确估算以满足机载告警等关键功能的可靠性要求；同时航空领域机器学习应用缺乏标准化流程，需要一种符合适航认证框架的学习保障方法。

Method: 本文提出了一套完整的机器学习发展流程，包括：(1)制定机器学习需求集合；(2)编写机器学习模型描述文档；(3)采用长短期记忆（LSTM）循环神经网络实现重量估算模型；(4)在传统航空电子计算机上对实现进行需求验证，确保满足ED-324/EASA标准要求。

Result: 基于空客全球在役机队大数据训练的LSTM模型在传统航电计算机上成功实现了重量估算功能，验证了机器学习模型在机载关键功能（如告警系统）上的部署兼容性与可行性。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了采用标准化学习保证流程的LSTM神经网络可以在现有航空电子硬件上有效估计直升机起飞重量，为机器学习在航空关键功能中的适航部署提供了实践范例。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the implementation of a novel supervised Machine Learning model for estimating helicopter weight during takeoff, utilizing extensive datasets from Airbus's global in-service fleet. The study details a learning assurance process aligned with the EASA concept paper for machine learning application, and with the on-going Eurocae ED-324. We propose a set of Machine Learning Requirements, a Machine Learning Model Description, and its implementation for a long short-term memory recurrent neural network. Finally, we verify the requirements on the implementation. Demonstrated on legacy avionics computers, the implementation is suitable for the deployment of the developed Machine Learning Model weight estimator on airborne targets for critical functions such as on-board alerting.

</details>


### [128] [Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19234)
*Eddy Soria,Aida Valls,Ana Beatriz Hernández-Lara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为d_TR的新型三角模糊数距离度量，该度量将线性重缩放（LRE）直接集成到距离计算中，避免了传统方法需要预先归一化步骤的问题。作者从数学上证明了d_TR满足度量公理（非负性、同一性、对称性和三角不等式），并具有有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性等优良性质，适用于异构模糊数据的多种应用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统中的决策常涉及模糊或不精确信息，通常用三角模糊数（TFN）表示。许多模糊方法需要量化TFN之间的距离，但现有距离度量假设所有值处于同一尺度，当应用于具有不同尺度或单位的异构属性时需要预先进行归一化。这一预处理要求增加了方法的复杂性和潜在误差，因此需要一种能够直接在距离计算中集成分归一化的度量方法。

Method: 本文提出将线性重缩放（LRE）直接融入距离计算中，定义了三角模糊数重缩放距离d_TR。作者形式化地证明了该距离满足度量的四个基本性质（非负性、同一性、对称性、三角不等式），并进一步证明了其具备有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性。此外，通过引入加权向量实现对不同维度的优先级排序。

Result: 论文从理论上证明了d_TR是一种真正的度量，满足非负性、同一性、对称性和三角不等式；同时证明了d_TR是有界的、尺度不变的和原点不变的。这些性质使得d_TR能够在无需预先归一化的条件下直接处理异构模糊数据。

Conclusion: d_TR由于具备上述数学性质并结合加权向量，非常适合应用于涉及异构模糊数据的场景，如合成指标构建、基于距离的机器学习算法以及多准则决策辅助等领域。

Abstract: Decision-making in complex systems often involves dealing with imprecise or uncertain information, frequently represented using fuzzy sets, particularly Triangular Fuzzy Numbers (TFNs). A crucial aspect of many fuzzy methods is the quantification of distance between TFNs. Many distance measures assume that all values are in the same scale, requiring a preliminary normalization stage when applied to heterogeneous attributes with different scales or units. This paper proposes the Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance (d_{TR}), a metric designed to address this challenge. The d_{TR} uniquely integrates Linear Rescaling (LRE) directly into the distance calculation, ensuring normalization during the comparison of fuzzy numbers. We formally prove that d_{TR} satisfies the properties of a metric, including non-negativity, identity, symmetry, and the triangle inequality. Furthermore, we demonstrate that d_{TR} is bounded, scale-invariant, and origin-invariant. These properties, combined with a weighting vector for prioritizing dimensions, make d_{TR} suitable for applications involving heterogeneous fuzzy data, such as the construction of synthetic indicators, distance-based machine learning algorithms or multicriteria-decision aiding.

</details>


### [129] [Holtercare-Bench: A Multimodal Benchmark for Evaluating Long-Term Dynamic ECG Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19297)
*Yihan Xie,Hanwen Cui,Runze Ye,Juekai Lin,Haoyang Wang,Jinhao Mao,Bo Zhang,Wenqiao Zhang,Xiaogang Guo,Jun Xiao,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Holtercare-23K大型多模态动态心电图数据集（含22,980个QA对，来自788份临床Holter记录，采用信号-视频-文本三模态对齐）及Holtercare-Bench基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在时间定位、临床诊断和全局总结方面的能力。研究发现现有MLLM在处理超长病理序列上存在显著性能差距，而微调可带来大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在医学应用中表现出色，但大多只处理静态图像或短期信号；在动态心电图的临床关键领域中，模型因缺乏高质量数据集和基准，难以进行复杂的时间推理和诊断报告生成。

Method: 构建Holtercare-23K数据集，从788份临床Holter记录中生成22,980个QA对，采用创新的信号-视频-文本三模态对齐方式；在此基础上设计Holtercare-Bench基准，从时间定位、临床诊断和全局总结三个维度评估模型；对代表性MLLM进行零样本评估和微调实验。

Result: 领先MLLM在零样本条件下处理超长病理序列时表现出显著性能差距；对代表性模型进行微调后性能获得大幅提升。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了当前MLLM在电生理学领域的局限性，为长期医学MLLM提供了基础性基准，并公开了项目代码。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel in medical applications, most of them favor static images or short-term signals. In the critical field of dynamic electrocardiograms (ECG), models struggle with complex temporal reasoning and diagnostic report generation due to a lack of high-quality datasets and benchmarks. To address this, we introduce (i) Holtercare-23K, a large-scale multimodal dynamic ECG dataset comprising 22,980 QA pairs derived from 788 clinical Holter records and featuring a novel signal-video-text tri-modal alignment. Based on this dataset, we present (ii) Holtercare-Bench, a multimodal benchmark that evaluates models on temporal localization, clinical diagnosis, and global summarization. Zero-shot evaluations of leading MLLMs reveal a significant performance gap in processing ultra-long pathological sequences. However, fine-tuning representative models yields substantial improvements. This work illuminates the limitations of current MLLMs in electrophysiology and provides a foundational benchmark for long-term medical MLLMs. Our project is available at https://github.com/ZJU4HealthCare/Holtercare-Bench.

</details>


### [130] [Quantum Kernel Estimation for the Discovery of Early Lung Cancer Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19304)
*Hamed Javidi,Alex Zajichek,Hakan Doga,Laxmi Parida,Filippo Utro,Peter J. Mazzone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了量子-经典混合机器学习方法在基于cfDNA片段组学和DNA甲基化的肺癌检测中的应用，通过角度与密集角度特征映射及多种纠缠策略构建量子核函数，并与经典SVM基线进行比较。结果显示量子核模型在片段组学数据上具有竞争力，部分20特征配置优于经典基线，但在甲基化数据上经典SVM表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低剂量CT肺癌筛查虽可降低死亡率，但受限于依从性和管理挑战；基于cfDNA的血检标志物虽具补充价值，但肺癌异质性和高维非线性分子信号使早期检测仍具挑战。作者旨在探索量子-经典混合机器学习能否更好地捕捉cfDNA片段组学和甲基化中的非线性模式，以提升肺癌检测性能。

Method: 采用DNA片段组学和DNA甲基化两个数据集，经特征选择后分别使用20和40特征子集训练模型。将特征编码到量子希尔伯特空间，采用角度（angle）和密集角度（dense-angle）两种特征映射，并配合多种纠缠策略；通过精确态矢量模拟计算基于保真度的量子核函数，集成到预计算核SVM和核PCA逻辑回归中，并与基于原始特征训练的经典SVM模型进行对比。

Result: 在重复留出评估中，量子核模型在两个数据集上均表现出有竞争力的性能。在片段组学数据上，多个20特征配置的AUC优于经典SVM基线，表明能有效捕捉cfDNA片段化的非线性结构；在甲基化数据上，经典SVM取得最高AUC，但部分量子模型仍具竞争力且在部分情况下提升了特异性。将特征从20增加到40并未一致提升性能，反而常增加变异性。

Conclusion: 量子核方法有望成为基于cfDNA肺癌检测的有前景方案，其性能取决于编码方式和纠缠设计的选择；未来需进一步优化量子特征映射策略以充分发挥量子机器学习在液体活检中的潜力。

Abstract: Lung cancer screening with low-dose chest computed tomography reduces mortality, but its impact is limited by uptake, adherence, and management challenges. Blood-based cell-free DNA (cfDNA) biomarkers offer a complementary approach, although early detection remains difficult because of lung cancer heterogeneity and high-dimensional, nonlinear molecular signals. We evaluated quantum-classical hybrid machine learning for lung cancer detection using DNA fragmentomics and DNA methylation. After feature selection, models were trained using 20- and 40-feature subsets. Features were encoded into quantum Hilbert space using angle and dense-angle feature maps with multiple entanglement strategies. Fidelity-based quantum kernels were computed with exact statevector simulation and integrated with precomputed-kernel SVM and kernel-PCA logistic regression and compared with an SVM model trained on the original features.
  This framework enabled systematic evaluation of how encoding and entanglement design affect classification. Across repeated held-out evaluations, quantum-kernel models achieved competitive performance on both datasets. For fragmentomics, several 20-feature configurations improved AUC relative to a classical SVM baseline, suggesting effective capture of nonlinear cfDNA fragmentation structure. For methylation, the classical SVM achieved the highest AUC, although selected quantum models remained competitive and improved specificity in some cases. Increasing features from 20 to 40 did not consistently improve performance and often increased variability. Overall, these results support quantum kernel methods as a promising approach for cfDNA-based lung cancer detection.

</details>


### [131] [Improved Confidence Estimates for Black-Box Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19323)
*Sokhna Diarra Mbacke,Mouloud Belbahri,Gabriel Loaiza-Ganem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用数据集提升LLM不确定性量化（UQ）性能的方法：将现有零样本UQ分数与相似查询的正确性作为特征，训练简单分类器预测LLM回答的正确性。该方法计算开销极小，能持续优于现有UQ方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定性量化对LLM安全部署至关重要，现有UQ方法（如口语化置信度、多代生成）多为零样本且无需标注数据即可量化不确定性，但在实际部署前仍需在目标数据集上评估其性能。现有方法未充分利用这一评估环节的数据信息。

Method: 利用目标数据集，构建简单分类器预测LLM回答的正确性。分类器输入特征包括：现有UQ分数，以及相似查询（similar queries）的正确性信息。该方法作为对现有UQ分数的直接增强，计算开销极小。

Result: 在多个数据集上，该方法持续（consistently）优于现有的零样本UQ方法，且仅引入极小的计算开销。

Conclusion: 通过在部署前利用数据集构建简单分类器，可以廉价且直接地增强LLM的不确定性量化性能，适用于实际应用场景。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for the safe deployment of large language models (LLMs). Existing methods, from verbalized confidence to ones requiring multiple generations, are often zero-shot and produce scores quantifying uncertainty without the need for labelled data. Nonetheless, in practice one must always evaluate their performance on a dataset of interest before deployment. In this work we show that, by leveraging this dataset, we consistently outperform these existing scores. Specifically, we build simple classifiers that predict LLM response correctness by using these scores and the correctness of similar queries as features. Our method produces minimal computational overhead, making it a cheap and straightforward enhancement for UQ in LLMs for real-world applications.

</details>


### [132] [Mechanistic Tomography: Designed Measurement for Control-Oriented Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19338)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"机制断层扫描"(mechanistic tomography)框架，将patching、梯度、Hessian-vector积和子集干预等机制可解释性测量统一为y = Ax + w的线性测量形式，并给出一个从低成本测量起步、在预期尺度上验证、校准偏差、必要时扩展测量族的实用流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性领域存在多种测量方法（patching、梯度、Hessian-vector积、子集干预），它们基于不同的访问假设、测量不同目标量，但缺乏一个统一的数学框架来理解它们的共同测量结构，也无法系统指导如何选择和组合测量方案。

Method: 提出mechanistic tomography框架：在选定基和干预族下，测量形式化为y = Ax + w，A描述干预矩阵，x为目标映射，w涵盖非线性响应、采样误差和基失配噪声。基于此给出实用程序：(1)从最小成本测量开始；(2)在预期尺度的held-out干预上测试；(3)校准简单失配；(4)当存在结构残差时扩展测量族。在多个场景中验证：两HMM控制场景、前向访问下的稀疏聚合测量、梯度访问下的有限探针、提升测量与Hessian-vector积、Tracr基依赖分析、GPT-2-small IOI和Qwen-2.5-7B上的实证。

Result: 在两HMM模型中，控制误差随观测器误差上升，且目标改进可能掩盖干扰状态移动；前向访问下稀疏聚合测量用比坐标patching更少的干预恢复有限效应映射；梯度访问下有限探针改进局部归因映射；提升测量和Hessian-vector积恢复一阶映射遗漏的交互，但Tracr表明所需测量族依赖于基。GPT-2-small IOI上，Name Mover-Negative Name Mover交互是三个测试跨组对中最大的held-out预测项；Qwen-2.5-7B上，有限校准使加性拒绝响应映射充分，held-out误差不支持成对提升。

Conclusion: 机制断层扫描提供了统一的测量语言和实用流程，帮助研究者选择合适测量族、最小化测量成本、并通过held-out干预验证估计质量；控制场景和多种模型上的实验证明了该框架的有效性和基/交互依赖的重要性。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability seeks quantities that models do not expose directly: represented states, component effects, interactions, and responses to interventions. Patching, gradients, Hessian-vector products, and subset interventions provide different measurements under different access assumptions and may target different quantities. We formulate their shared measurement structure as mechanistic tomography: designed measurement for recovering internal mechanisms and intervention effects.
  For a chosen basis and intervention family, measurements take the form y = Ax + w, where A describes the interventions, x is the target map, and w contains nonlinear response, sampling error, and basis misspecification. This language gives a practical procedure: start with the least costly measurements, test on held-out interventions at the intended scale, calibrate simple mismatch, and expand the measurement family when structured residuals remain.
  Control provides a demanding validation setting because an estimate that guides an intervention acts as an observer. In a two-HMM model, control error rises with observer error, while target improvement can hide nuisance-state movement. Under forward-only access, sparse aggregate measurements recover a finite-effect map with fewer interventions than coordinate patching. With gradient access, finite probes improve a local attribution map. Lifted measurements and Hessian-vector products recover interactions missed by first-order maps, while Tracr shows that the required family depends on the basis. On GPT-2-small IOI, the Name Mover-Negative Name Mover interaction is the largest held-out predictive term among three tested cross-group pairs. On Qwen-2.5-7B, finite calibration makes an additive refusal-response map adequate, so held-out error does not support pairwise lifting.

</details>


### [133] [Uncovering the Limits of Proof Sharing for Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19351)
*Kanak Das,Shubham Ugare,Bor-Yuh Evan Chang,Sasa Misailovic,Gagandeep Singh,Manu Sridharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于模板的加速技术（template-based acceleration）在神经网络鲁棒性验证中的有效性及其局限，提出了一种新的"联合稳定神经元"（jointly stable neurons）指标来解释模板包含率（subsumption rates）在不同场景下的差异，并进一步提出了FastCert技术，自动在神经网络各层间分配模板以提升性能影响，在基于覆盖设计的L0验证任务上平均获得1.13倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络的鲁棒性验证因其在关键领域的应用而日益重要。已有的基于证明共享（proof sharing）的不完全验证加速技术通过跨查询复用中间层抽象状态（即模板）来加速验证，但模板加速的效果在不同网络架构、属性、数据集和训练方法下的鲁棒性尚不明确，需要系统性研究其有效性和极限。

Method: 本文首先对模板加速在不同场景下的有效性进行系统性研究，提出"联合稳定神经元"这一新颖指标来解释模板包含率在不同场景下的广泛差异。基于该分析，提出了FastCert技术，能够自动地在神经网络各层间分配模板以提升性能影响，并在模板不太可能产生加速的场景下完全弃用模板。

Result: 研究表明模板包含率在不同场景下可能存在巨大差异，在部分情况下模板技术几乎不可能带来任何加速。FastCert在大量基于覆盖设计（covering-design based）的L0验证任务上，相对于现有的模板复用技术平均获得了1.13倍的加速。

Conclusion: 模板加速技术并非在所有场景下都有效，其效果依赖于具体的网络和验证任务特性。通过提出的联合稳定神经元指标可以预测模板加速的有效性，而FastCert通过智能地分配或弃用模板，能够在更广泛的场景下稳定提升验证性能。

Abstract: Robustness verification of neural networks is increasingly important, due to their use in many critical domains. In certain scenarios, proof sharing has been shown to accelerate incomplete verification techniques by reusing intermediate-layer abstract states, or templates, across queries. However, questions remain as to the robustness of template-based acceleration across varying network architectures, properties, datasets, and training methods. In this work, we perform a systematic study of the effectiveness of template-based acceleration and its limits. Our study shows that template subsumption rates can vary widely across scenarios. We present a novel metric of jointly stable neurons to explain this variation, showing that in some cases template-based techniques are very unlikely to provide any speedup. Then, we present FastCert, a novel technique for automatically distributing templates across neural network layers to increase performance impact, eschewing templates entirely if they are unlikely to produce a speedup. Across a large set of covering-design based $L_0$-verification tasks, FastCert achieved an average speedup of 1.13x over an extant template-based reuse technique.

</details>


### [134] [Longitudinal Bayesian Learning of Continuous Disease Position across the Alzheimer's Disease Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19436)
*Yingying Zhang,Kun Zhao,Guodong Liu,Qi Huang,Pengfei Gu,Dongchul Kim,Erik Enriquez,Alex D. Leow,Paul M. Thompson,Heng Huang,Hongchang Gao,Liang Zhan,Haoteng Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Disease Continuum Positioning (DCP)，一种纵向贝叶斯学习框架，利用纵向弥散张量成像（DTI）连续估算阿尔茨海默病（AD）的疾病严重程度。通过将疾病严重程度建模为低维概率隐变量，并结合弱临床监督，推导出Disease Continuum Score (DCS)量化个体在AD连续谱上的位置及不确定性。在ADNI队列上的实验表明DCP优于代表性疾病进展方法，且DCS能准确刻画疾病严重程度、保持纵向演化并预测未来疾病转化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病本质上是连续渐进的生物学过程，但现有基于神经影像的AI方法大多局限于横断面影像的离散诊断或临床评分预测，无法捕捉疾病的连续演进特性，缺乏对疾病严重程度的连续量化表征。

Method: 提出DCP框架：将疾病严重程度建模为低维概率潜变量，通过纵向贝叶斯学习整合纵向DTI观测与弱临床监督信号；在此基础上推导出Disease Continuum Score (DCS)，用于量化个体在AD连续谱上的位置，同时提供不确定性估计。

Result: 在ADNI队列上的广泛实验表明：DCP一致优于代表性疾病进展方法；验证分析显示DCS能够准确刻画疾病严重程度、展现强临床相关性、保持纵向疾病演化模式，并可预测未来疾病转化。

Conclusion: DCS提供了一种定量、影像衍生的连续疾病表征，超越传统诊断标签和临床评分，支持对阿尔茨海默病进展的连续评估，为AD精准监测和临床决策提供了新工具。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) progresses as a continuous biological process, whereas most existing neuroimaging-based artificial intelligence methods remain limited to discrete diagnosis or clinical score prediction from cross-sectional imaging. In this work, we propose Disease Continuum Positioning (DCP), a longitudinal Bayesian Learning framework that continuously estimates disease severity from longitudinal diffusion tensor imaging (DTI). Specifically, DCP models disease severity as a low-dimensional probabilistic latent variable by jointly integrating longitudinal observations with weak clinical supervision, from which the proposed Disease Continuum Score (DCS) is derived to quantify an individual's position along the Alzheimer's disease continuum together with its associated uncertainty. Extensive experiments on the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) cohort demonstrate that DCP consistently outperforms representative disease progression methods. More importantly, comprehensive validation analyses show that DCS accurately characterizes disease severity, exhibits strong clinical relevance, preserves longitudinal disease evolution, and predicts future disease conversion. These results suggest that DCS provides a quantitative imaging-derived representation for continuous assessment of Alzheimer's disease progression beyond conventional diagnostic labels and clinical scores.

</details>


### [135] [Quantifying Event Impacts on Time Series via Multiscale Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19447)
*Yiming Sun,Shengyu Chen,Zhengzhang Chen,Haoyu Wang,Xiaowei Jia,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EventTime是一个多分辨率框架，用于估计网络安全事件披露后的短期异常财务损失。它结合长短期市场上下文和事件元数据，通过事件融合模块和动态对比学习缓解稀疏监督问题，并在新构建的SECURE数据集上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全事件等外部冲击会通过新闻等渠道传播并突发性中断金融市场时间序列，造成异常损失。然而现有时间序列预测模型主要关注趋势、季节性、自相关等内生规律，难以处理稀有且异质的外部事件；同时高影响事件稀疏且背景市场噪声大，使得事件条件下的损失估计极具挑战性。

Method: EventTime采用多分辨率框架，融合长时域市场背景、短时域事件前动态以及事件元数据。核心组件包括：(1) 事件融合模块，将时间序列表示与事件属性耦合以识别相关近期市场模式；(2) 动态对比学习目标，构建事件和时间序列感知的正负样本对以缓解稀疏监督问题。此外构建了SECURE数据集，将网络安全事件与股票市场时间序列及结构化/LLM衍生语义特征对齐。

Result: 实验表明EventTime在估计事件后财务损失方面一致优于最先进的时序预测和事件感知基线。进一步分析显示其具有更强的事件敏感表示、对不完整元数据更高的鲁棒性，以及更可解释的短期市场影响估计。

Conclusion: EventTime有效解决了事件条件下短期异常损失预测问题，通过多分辨率建模和动态对比学习，在稀疏高影响事件场景下实现了更准确、更鲁棒且可解释的市场影响估计，为网络安全披露事件的金融影响分析提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Shocks that spread through the web, such as cybersecurity breach disclosures, can abruptly disrupt financial time series and cause substantial abnormal losses. While these events are disclosed as discrete records through news reports, regulatory filings, or public databases, their consequences unfold through continuous market dynamics. This creates an event-conditioned impact prediction problem: given pre-event market history and limited event metadata, the goal is to estimate short-term post-disclosure abnormal loss rather than reconstruct the full post-event trajectory. However, most time-series forecasting models focus on endogenous regularities such as trend, seasonality, and autocorrelation, and thus struggle with rare and heterogeneous external events. The challenge is further amplified by sparse high-impact events and background market noise. We introduce EventTime, a multi-resolution framework that combines long-horizon market context, short-horizon pre-event dynamics, and event metadata. It incorporates an event fusion module that couples temporal representations with event attributes to identify relevant recent market patterns. To mitigate sparse supervision, EventTime further introduces a dynamic contrastive objective that constructs event- and time-series-aware positive and negative pairs during training. We also construct SECURE, a real-world dataset aligning cybersecurity incidents with stock-market time series and structured and LLM-derived semantic features. Experiments show that EventTime consistently outperforms state-of-the-art time-series and event-aware baselines in estimating post-event financial losses. Further analyses demonstrate more event-sensitive representations, greater robustness to incomplete metadata, and more interpretable estimates of short-term market impact following cybersecurity disclosures.

</details>


### [136] [LLM as Detector: An In-context Learning Approach for Tabular Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19463)
*Tu Anh Hoang Nguyen,Dang Nguyen,Thuc Duy Le,Trung Le,Sunil Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LLM-Detector框架，利用LLM的上下文学习能力从正常数据中编码结构化知识，通过代码生成构建评分引擎，实现无需微调的表格数据异常检测，在24个数据集上超越15个SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据中的异常样本往往是跨特征依赖关系的违反，而非简单的边际偏差。现有检测器依赖几何或重建信号，而基于LLM的方法要么需要微调LLM，要么依赖生成合成异常，存在计算成本高或效果受限的问题。

Method: 将正常训练数据转换为统计摘要、因果关系和蒸馏原型，组织成提示词用于代码生成，生成评分引擎来综合评估统计偏差、结构不一致性和基于密度的异常，为每个测试样本计算异常分数。

Result: 在24个表格数据集上与15个SOTA基线对比，在混合类型和纯连续型数据设置下均取得一致的改进，且无需LLM微调或神经网络训练，降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: LLM-Detector通过提示词条件下的结构化评分合成，充分挖掘LLM的上下文学习能力来推导异常检测逻辑，同时消除了微调和网络训练的需求，为实际表格系统的异常检测提供了高效可行的方案。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in tabular data is challenging because abnormal samples often arise as violations of cross-feature dependencies rather than simple marginal deviations. Existing detectors rely on geometric or reconstruction signals, while prior LLM-based approaches mainly fine-tune LLMs with normal samples or generate synthetic anomalies. We propose LLM-Detector, a framework that utilizes the in-context learning capacity of LLMs for structured, prompt-conditioned scoring synthesis, enabling LLMs to derive anomaly detection logic from structured normal-state knowledge. Specifically, normal training data are converted into statistical summaries, causal dependencies, and distilled prototypes that are organized into a prompt for code generation. The resulting scoring engine evaluates statistical deviation, structural inconsistency, and density-based abnormality then computes an anomaly score for each test sample. We evaluate LLM-Detector on 24 tabular datasets, comparing against 15 SOTA baselines. Results show consistent improvements across both mixed-type and continuous-only settings. Moreover, this design eliminates the need for LLM fine-tuning or neural network training, reducing computational cost and enabling practical anomaly detection in real-world tabular systems.

</details>


### [137] [When to Retrain: An Empirical Study of Retraining Policies for Streaming ML Under Concept Drift, Budget, and Latency Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19488)
*Sawan Dasari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过3,933次受控实验，系统研究了机器学习生产系统在概念漂移下的三种模型刷新策略（周期性重训练、错误阈值触发和基于ADWIN的统计漂移触发）与不重训练基线的对比。核心发现是：最关键的决策并非重训练策略本身，而是部署模型是否进行增量学习。当采用增量更新时，所有策略与基线无显著差异；不采用增量更新时，策略间存在15-55个百分点的准确率差距，周期性重训练在突变和渐变漂移下显著优于反应式策略，反应式策略仅在循环漂移下保有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的机器学习系统在概念漂移下性能会下降，但实践者对何时重训练缺乏有原则性的指导。重训练具有成本高、预算有限且存在训练与部署延迟（旧模型持续服务）等约束，现有研究缺乏在统一系统模型下对这些约束进行显式建模的受控实证比较。

Method: 构建统一系统模型，显式建模重训练预算和训练加部署延迟；设计三种实用模型刷新策略（周期性重训练、错误阈值触发、ADWIN统计漂移触发）与不重训练基线对比；在3,933次实验运行中，跨三种漂移机制、三个预算水平、最多五个延迟水平、三个数据集和两种学习模式进行系统评估。

Result: 增量学习是决定性的设计因素：在逐样本增量更新下，54个配对比较中没有任何策略与基线存在实际显著差异（即使极端延迟）；无增量更新时，策略选择导致15-55个百分点的漂移后准确率差异，周期性重训练在突变和渐变漂移下显著优于两种反应式策略，反应式策略仅在循环漂移下占优。此外发现反应式策略的系统性失败模式和延迟-预算排队交互作用可使有效重训练预算减半。

Conclusion: 重训练策略的选择重要性远低于是否采用增量学习这一更基础的设计决策；在非增量场景下，简单周期性重训练是最稳健的策略；反应式策略存在系统性缺陷和排队交互导致的预算静默损耗；作者发布完整模拟器、数据管道和逐次运行产物以确保可复现性。

Abstract: Production machine learning systems degrade under concept drift, yet practitioners have little principled guidance on when to retrain. Retraining is costly, retraining budgets are finite, and a retrained model does not take effect instantly: training and deployment latency leave a stale model serving predictions while the data continues to move. We present a controlled empirical study of three practical model-refresh policies (periodic retraining, error-threshold triggering, and statistical drift-triggered retraining with ADWIN) against a no-retrain baseline, evaluated under a unified system model that makes retraining budgets and training-plus-deployment latency explicit. Across 3,933 experiment runs spanning three drift regimes, three budget levels, up to five latency levels, three datasets, and two learning modes, we find that the single most consequential design decision is not the retraining policy but whether the deployed model learns incrementally. With per-sample incremental updates, and for the linear online learner with immediate labels studied here, no policy differs from the no-retrain baseline by a practically significant margin in any of 54 paired comparisons, even at extreme latency. Without incremental updates, policy choice separates outcomes by 15-55 percentage points of post-drift accuracy, and simple periodic retraining significantly outperforms both reactive policies under abrupt and gradual drift, while reactive policies retain an advantage only under recurring drift. We document systematic failure modes of reactive policies and a latency-budget queueing interaction that silently halves effective retraining budgets, and release the full simulator, dataset pipelines, and per-run artifacts for reproducibility.

</details>


### [138] [DeltaMomentum: A Key-Value based Anisotropic Momentum Update via Delta Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19491)
*Euijin Hong,Guannan Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DeltaMomentum，将方向感知融入动量更新规则本身，利用线性层梯度的键值结构，通过canonical delta rule更新动量缓冲，使每个方向按其出现频率被遗忘。作为任意优化器的即插即用动量缓冲，在FineWeb-Edu预训练中，AdamW+DeltaMomentum（DeltaAdamW）最多可减少46%的步数达到相同验证损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代优化器的动量是梯度的指数移动平均（EMA），以固定速率遗忘所有方向。然而深度网络训练中输入的梯度方向是高度各向异性的——少数方向频繁出现，多数方向稀见。现有方法通过在动量缓冲外包裹额外处理结构来应对各向异性，但动量更新规则本身未变，作者认为应将方向感知直接构建进动量更新中。

Method: 发现线性层梯度可分解为输入（作为键key）和输出侧误差（作为值value）两部分。利用这种键值结构，DeltaMomentum用canonical delta rule更新动量缓冲，使每个方向的遗忘速率由其出现频率决定。理论上证明其是有效动量，可在无需矩阵求逆的情况下应用输入侧曲率校正，且固定/漂移最优点下都比EMA更快清除过时方向。作为动量缓冲可即插即用，系数在μP下可跨宽度迁移。

Result: 在FineWeb-Edu预训练中，DeltaAdamW相比AdamW达到相同验证损失最多节省46.39±4.32%步数（67M规模）和22.12±0.80%（370M），1B规模在Chinchilla最优预算下增益保持。在两种语言模型规模下优于调优的Muon基线。SGD、ResNet-18和ViT-Tiny在CIFAR-10上增益同样成立。额外计算仅为gated-MLP块线性成本的22.2%~25.0%，无持久内存开销。训练诊断证实了预测机制：更好的梯度跟踪和更健康的输入方向。

Conclusion: DeltaMomentum通过将方向感知内建到动量更新规则中，有效解决了深度学习中梯度方向各向异性的问题，在语言模型预训练和图像分类上均显著优于现有优化器，且计算开销可接受、可即插即用、支持宽度迁移，是一种具有理论和实证双重支撑的通用动量改进方案。

Abstract: Most modern optimizers form their momentum as an exponential moving average (EMA) of past gradients, forgetting every direction at one fixed rate. However, the inputs a deep network sees during training can be highly anisotropic, with a few directions queried frequently while most are seen rarely. Recent methods address this anisotropy by wrapping extra processing around this buffer, leaving the momentum update itself unchanged. We propose DeltaMomentum, which builds direction-awareness into the momentum update rule. The main observation is that the gradient of a linear layer splits into an input that acts as a key and an output-side error that acts as a value. Exploiting the key-value structure, DeltaMomentum updates the momentum buffer by the canonical delta rule, so each direction is forgotten at a rate set by how often it appears. We prove that it is a valid momentum, that it applies the input-side curvature correction without matrix inversion, and that it clears stale directions faster than EMA under both a fixed and a drifting optimum. It is a drop-in replacement for the momentum buffer of any optimizer, its coefficient transfers across widths under $μ$P, and its extra compute stays between $22.2\%$ and $25.0\%$ of a gated-MLP block's linear cost with no persistent memory. In FineWeb-Edu pretraining, AdamW with DeltaMomentum (DeltaAdamW) reaches AdamW's validation loss in up to $46.39 \pm 4.32\%$ fewer steps at 67M and $22.12 \pm 0.80\%$ at 370M over three seeds, and the gain persists at 1B on a Chinchilla-optimal budget. A Muon baseline tuned under the same protocol sits above DeltaAdamW at both language-model scales, and the gain holds for SGD, ResNet-18, and ViT-Tiny on CIFAR-10. Training-time diagnostics confirm the predicted mechanism, better gradient tracking and healthier input directions.

</details>


### [139] [Beyond Multimodal Alignment: Certifying Physical Language through Response Substitution and Ordered Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19492)
*Kaizhen Tan,Xin Xu,Siru Tao,Yixiao Li,Hanzhe Hong,Yang Feng,Heqing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个操作性能力层级和"不相交桥算子替换证书"(DBOSC)，用于检验多模态世界中不同传感器是否携带相同的可执行语义，以及该语义能否在新的动作组合下存活。通过触觉、音频和加速度表征的实验证明响应替换性和有序执行是可分离、可分别测试的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型日益将紧凑的多模态表征作为感知与物理交互之间的接口，但现有探针无法确定不同传感器是否携带相同的可执行含义，也无法确定该含义能否在新的动作组合中存活。缺乏一种机制来区分属性访问、响应替换、融合闭合和有序执行等不同层次的能力。

Method: 引入操作性能力层级和DBOSC（Disjoint-Bridge Operator-Substitution Certificate），检验独立训练的多模态编译器能否在冻结的响应图上、基于训练面板之外的证据进行互换。实验在两个系统上进行：Cluster Haptic触觉-音频-加速度系统（19个未见表面）和受控弹塑性系统（预注册预算和收敛预算两种条件下的有序执行测试）。

Result: 在Cluster Haptic上，相同未见表面的音频和加速度表征在响应空间中的距离比错误表面对的距离近4.5倍，19个未见表面全部保持该差距。在弹塑性系统中，预注册预算下前置条件拒绝堆栈（冻结执行器无法在保留程序上前进）；收敛预算下秩三图表能执行程序（oracle NMSE 0.18），融合优于单一模态，16项注册检查中14项通过；两个失败源于融合信息矩阵的对角限制与全矩阵表现相同。此外，发出整体程序而非共享逐步动力学的执行器比实体盲预测器差38倍。

Conclusion: 通过门控是执行器的属性而非图表的属性；压缩和融合本身无法确定未见过的组合法则（存在非可辨识性结果）。属性访问、响应替换、融合闭合和有序执行是四个截然不同、可分别测试的能力成就。

Abstract: World models increasingly treat compact multimodal representations as interfaces between perception and physical interaction, yet existing probes do not establish whether different sensors carry the same executable meaning or whether that meaning survives a new action composition. We introduce an operational capability hierarchy and the Disjoint-Bridge Operator-Substitution Certificate (DBOSC), which asks whether independently trained modality compilers enter a frozen response chart interchangeably on evidence outside their training panels. On Cluster Haptic, audio and acceleration representations of the same unseen surface are 4.5x closer in response space than wrong-surface pairings, with the gap holding for all 19 held-out surfaces; unsealing withheld responses confirms that every branch predicts the physics better than the population chart. We then test ordered execution in a controlled elastoplastic system with complementary modality blind spots. At the pre-registered budget, the prerequisite refuses the stack because the frozen executor cannot advance even an exact chart coordinate through a held-out program. At a converged budget, the same rank-three chart executes those programs (oracle NMSE 0.18), fusion improves on both modalities, and 14 of 16 registered checks pass; the two failures arise because a diagonal restriction of the fused information matrix performs as well as the full one. Clearing the gate is a property of the executor, not the chart: an executor emitting whole programs instead of shared per-step dynamics is 38x worse than an entity-blind predictor on the same chart. A matching non-identifiability result explains why compression and fusion alone cannot determine an unseen composition law. These results separate attribute access, response substitution, fusion closure, and ordered execution into distinct, separately testable achievements.

</details>


### [140] [Empirical Characterization of Learning Geometry in Hybrid Quantum Forecasting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19497)
*Sandra Leticia Juárez-Osorio,Jorge I. Hernandez-Martinez,Jesus Ivan Ruiz-Martinez,Andres Mendez-Vazquez,Eduardo Rodriguez-Tello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过结构对齐的经典基线对比，刻画了紧凑混合量子预测模型的学习动态，发现尽管两者的优化几何（NTK诊断）存在明显差异，但下游测试性能相当；混合模型参数量更少（125 vs 281）且在18个频率条件中的15个更早达到验证检查点，说明可比的泛化可以从截然不同的学习轨迹中涌现，单个NTK诊断不能单调预测验证收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究往往仅以端点精度对比量子与经典模型的优劣，忽略训练过程中的学习动力学差异。作者希望从NTK（神经正切核）视角揭示混合量子模型与经典模型在优化几何上的本质区别，从而判断“量子优势”之外是否存在架构依赖的学习行为，并评估NTK诊断指标作为泛化预测工具的有效性。

Method: 采用两种基准：平稳谐波混合信号与非平稳啁啾信号，控制频谱复杂度和数据量。将紧凑混合量子预测模型与结构对齐的经典基线（以及傅里叶增强经典基线和控制的重上传消融）进行对比，分析经验NTK动力学，包括核-目标对齐、核漂移、谱集中度和训练损失等指标。

Result: 经典模型具有更强的早期目标对齐；混合模型通常表现出更不集中的核谱和更小的核漂移。两种架构在所有评估场景下达到相似的未见过数据性能。混合模型仅用125个可训练参数（经典为281个），且在18个频率条件中的15个更早到达验证选择检查点。傅里叶增强基线无法复现混合模型的训练行为，重上传消融表明重复编码系统性改变了优化和核几何。

Conclusion: 可比的泛化性能可以从显著不同的学习轨迹中涌现；单个NTK诊断指标无法作为验证收敛的单调预测器。研究并非宣称普遍的量子优势，而是识别出被端点精度掩盖的架构依赖型学习行为。

Abstract: We characterize the learning dynamics of a compact hybrid quantum forecasting model through comparison with a structurally aligned classical baseline. Using stationary harmonic-mixture and nonstationary chirp benchmarks with controlled spectral complexity and data availability, we analyze empirical Neural Tangent Kernel dynamics through kernel-target alignment, kernel drift, spectral concentration, and training loss. The classical model exhibits stronger early target alignment, whereas the hybrid model generally develops a less concentrated kernel spectrum and smaller kernel drift. Despite these distinct optimization geometries, both architectures attain similar held-out performance across the evaluated regimes. Notably, the hybrid model uses 125 trainable parameters compared with 281 for the classical baseline and reaches its validation-selected checkpoint earlier in 15 of 18 frequency conditions. A Fourier-augmented classical baseline does not reproduce the observed training behavior, while a controlled re-uploading ablation shows that repeated encoding systematically modifies both optimization and kernel geometry. These results demonstrate that comparable generalization can emerge from substantially different learning trajectories and that individual NTK diagnostics do not provide monotonic predictors of validation convergence. Rather than claiming a general quantum advantage, the study identifies architecture-dependent learning behavior that is masked by endpoint accuracy alone.

</details>


### [141] [In Two Minds about Lifelong Learning: Exploring Hemispheric Redundancy and Specialisation in Neural Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19514)
*Benjamin Smith,Levin Kuhlmann,Kaushik Roy,Gideon Kowadlo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出4MAS（四模块清醒/睡眠）宏架构，借鉴动物记忆巩固的三个关键概念——经验重放、REM睡眠和大脑不对称性，用于解决机器学习中的持续学习难题，在Split-MNIST（98.3%）、Split-Fashion-MNIST（84.9%）和Split-CIFAR-100（29.29%）数据集上取得了具有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习算法在持续学习上面临重大挑战：算法需要在保留已有知识和适应新数据模式之间进行权衡，缺乏持续学习能力时需用全部数据重新训练，但在数据不可得（存储限制、成本或隐私限制）时此方法不可行。而生物系统能够持续学习而不经历灾难性遗忘，因此有必要借鉴生物学习机制来构建更优的持续学习框架。

Method: 提出4MAS（4 Module Awake/Sleep）宏架构，它包含两个不对称半球，每个半球拥有各自的长期和短期记忆机制，并在增量学习任务之间引入"睡眠"周期以促进记忆巩固。架构整合了三个核心概念：经验重放、REM睡眠和双侧性（bilaterality），建模了与记忆巩固相关的神经组件和状态。

Result: 4MAS架构在三个基准数据集上取得了有竞争力的准确率：Split-MNIST为98.3%，Split-Fashion-MNIST为84.9%，Split-CIFAR-100为29.29%。

Conclusion: 通过模拟动物记忆巩固的高层机制（不对称半球、长短时记忆分工、清醒与睡眠交替），4MAS展示了机器学习模型可以从生物持续学习原理中获益的可行性，为设计更稳健的持续学习系统提供了新方向。

Abstract: Persistent intelligent systems require the ability to learn continually, but current machine learning approaches face significant challenges in this area compared to biological learning systems. Machine learning algorithms typically trade off retention of previously learned information and adaptation to new or changing data patterns. When continual learning capabilities are absent, algorithms must undergo retraining using the entire data set, an approach that becomes impractical when original training data are unavailable due to storage constraints, financial or computational costs, or privacy restrictions. However, biological animals can learn continually, without experiencing catastrophic forgetting. This paper attempts to build a high-level framework for how animals learn and preserve knowledge by modelling neural components and states that are known to be related to memory consolidation. We focus on three concepts: experience replay, REM sleep, and bilaterality. We propose 4MAS (4 Module Awake/Sleep), a novel macroarchitecture demonstrating how machine learning models might benefit from asymmetric hemispheres, each with their own long- and short-term memory mechanisms, and how a period of sleep between incremental learning tasks might benefit memory consolidation. Finally, we present results showing that our architecture achieves competitive results on the Split-MNIST, Split-Fashion-MNIST and Split-CIFAR-100 datasets, with 98.3%, 84.9%, and 29.29% accuracy respectively.

</details>


### [142] [Continuous Adversarial MeanFlow Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19540)
*Yara Bahram,Zahra Dehghani,Mélodie Desbos,Eric Granger,Pablo Piantanida,Mohammadhadi Shateri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MeanFlow-Transfer (MF-T)框架，将异构预训练扩散/流模型统一为共享速度表示进行少数据域适配与加速，并提出Continuous Adversarial MeanFlow (CAMF)后训练阶段，将对抗精细化的适用范围从瞬时速度扩展到MeanFlow的有限区间平均速度，在5个目标域上以最高125×更少的NFE达到或超过微调教师模型的FID/FDD性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作面临两个核心问题：(1) 将预训练扩散/流模型适配到新域时，多步采样成本高且已有的加速方法被绑定在特定的源参数化形式（ε、x、v、u）上，异构预训练模型缺乏统一的加速目标；(2) 对抗细化虽然能在少步生成中有效提升质量，但仅针对瞬时速度流，无法适用于MeanFlow (MF)模型预测的有限区间平均速度。

Method: 两阶段方法：(1) MeanFlow-Transfer：将异构源模型输出映射到统一的MeanFlow速度表示，用源权重初始化MF生成器，在目标域上优化MF目标，从而在单个训练循环中统一适配与加速；(2) Continuous Adversarial MeanFlow (CAMF)：后训练阶段，将连续对抗流模型从瞬时速度推广到MF的有限区间平均速度，通过对比真实与预测区间端点的可学习势能变化来恢复MF回归平均所丢失的细节，且在区间趋于零时退化为瞬时准则。
